MSCI 降評風險升高，印尼股市保守看待
2026 年 10 月 27 日
一、 市場動態
1. MSCI 擬將印尼降至獨立市場，降評風險明顯升高：MSCI 於 10 月 15 日宣布，啟動
將印尼股市由「新興市場（Emerging Market）」 直接降至「獨立市場（Standalone
Market）」的諮詢程序，意見徵詢將於 2027 年 4 月 30 日截止，最晚於 2027 年 6 月
30 日公布最終決定。MSCI 原先於今年 1 月提出可能降至「邊境市場（Frontier Market
）」，此次則跳過「邊境市場」提出直接將該國股市降至「獨立市場」的方案，顯示對

印尼市場治理的疑慮進一步加深。雖然「邊境市場」仍可能是替代分類方案，但 MSCI
強調，除非有明確證據顯示市場治理及運作出現實質改善，否則預期將依目前提案重
新分類，顯示此次降評風險較過去顯著升高。
2. 股權透明度與交易秩序問題為 MSCI 主要疑慮：MSCI 指出，印尼股市長期存在股權
結構不透明、實際自由流通股比例難以確認、疑似協調交易，以及市場監理執法成效
不足等問題，影響國際投資人對市場公平性與可投資性的信心。雖然印尼主管機關已
提出強化持股超過 1%股東資訊揭露、建立高持股集中度（HSC）監理機制，以及規
劃逐步將最低自由流通股比例提高至 15%等改革措施，但 MSCI 認為相關改革尚未展
現持續且具實質性的改善成果。由於問題涉及市場制度與治理結構，改善所需時間可
能較一般短期市場波動更長。
3. 股市反彈後再度回落，外資撤離壓力仍高：受降評消息衝擊，印尼雅加達綜合指數 10
月 16 日重挫 11.57%，創 1998 年以來最大單日跌幅，19 日再跌 3.64%，盤中最低

觸及 4,907.37 點。雖然 20 日至 23 日連續四個交易日反彈，但 26 日再度下跌 1.22%
，收於 5,373.09 點，各產業類股全面收黑，顯示市場信心仍不穩定。根據 Reuters
報導，截至 10 月 19 日，2026 年外資已累計淨賣超印尼股票約 48.8 億美元。此外，
印尼證交所 26 日宣布，將引進獨立專家及外國投資人代表參與高持股集中度審查，
並自 11 月起加強調查疑似協調交易，必要時凍結可疑帳戶，以回應 MSCI 對市場制
度的疑慮。
二、 下跌原因分析：
1. 股市：潛在指數剔除與資金外流，持續壓抑市場評價
若印尼股市最終降至「獨立市場」，原則上將不再納入 MSCI 新興市場及邊境市場相關
指數，追蹤相關指數的被動式基金將須依規則調整持股，實際處理方式仍以 MSCI 後
續公告為準。若改降至「邊境市場」，則可能獲得部分邊境市場資金承接，衝擊相對較
小。Reuters 引述多家金融機構估計，印尼股市降至獨立市場可能引發約 30 億至 60

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
億美元被動資金流出，其中 UBS 估計約 40 億美元，並指出主動型基金另有約 150 億
美元潛在撤資風險，惟實際規模仍視最終分類結果及基金策略而定。
2. 匯率：外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力
印尼股市的降評風險不僅衝擊股市，也可能透過外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力。印尼
盾 10 月 16 日盤中一度貶至 18,070 兌 1 美元，為 7 月下旬以來最弱水準之一，當日
收盤約 18,000。若後續國際資金持續流出，可能進一步增加股匯市波動，對以外幣計
價、且未完全避險的印尼基金及 ETF 形成額外壓力。雖然印尼央行可透過匯市干預及

流動性管理措施穩定金融市場，但在資金外流壓力尚未緩解前，印尼盾仍可能維持偏
弱格局。
3. 基金與 ETF：指數權重下滑，單一市場曝險風險升高
MSCI 相關指數調整已對印尼股票產生實質影響。Reuters 引用 LSEG 資料顯示，印尼
在 MSCI 新興市場指數的權重已由 2026 年初的 1.16%降至 9 月底的 0.43%，相關成
分股亦由年初 18 檔減至 9 檔，反映部分指數資金調整壓力已提前出現。對印尼單一
國家基金及 ETF 而言，主要風險來自股市下跌、資金流出及印尼盾波動；主動式基金
雖可透過選股降低部分影響，但仍難以完全迴避整體市場風險。不同產品實際受到的
影響，仍應依追蹤指數、主要持股及匯率避險安排個別評估。
三、 市場看法：
1. 制度性問題難以速解，降評風險將持續牽動市場：我們認為，此次 MSCI 降評風險並
非單純反映經濟成長放緩或企業獲利不佳，而是涉及市場透明度、交易公平性與監理
執法等結構性問題。尤其 MSCI 不僅啟動降評諮詢，更持續限制印尼股票的部分指數

調整，包括不提高外資納入因子、不新增相關成分股，以及不進行向上規模分類調整
，顯示市場可投資性疑慮尚未獲得有效解決。儘管政府試圖推動改革，但制度修正仍
須經過執行與成效驗證，才能重新取得國際投資人的信任，因此短期內降評風險仍難
完全消除。
2. 外資撤離與指數權重下降，市場信心修復恐需較長時間：印尼今年以來外資已大幅賣
超，MSCI 新興市場指數中的權重亦明顯下降，顯示國際投資人對當地市場的疑慮已
逐漸反映於實際資金流向。我們認為，即使印尼最終成功避免降評，部分國際機構投
資人仍可能因市場透明度、流動性及治理品質疑慮而維持較低曝險。此外，MSCI 對
部分指數調整的凍結措施尚未解除，短期內新增指數資金流入機會仍受到限制，預期
國際資金回流與市場評價修復均可能需要較長時間。
3. 監理改革力度加大，但尚不足以扭轉保守展望：印尼政府近期持續加強改革，包括提
高資訊透明度、強化交易監控、對違規交易開罰，以及引進外部獨立人士參與監理審
查。10 月 26 日該國政府進一步提出主動調查疑似協調交易及凍結可疑帳戶等措施，

11 月 2 日起亦規劃將高持股集中度股票排除於雅加達綜合指數之外。相關改革有助改

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
善交易秩序及短期市場信心，但部分措施仍待正式實施，且實際成效尚未獲得 MSCI
認可。考量制度性問題改善需要時間，政策利多雖可能帶動短線反彈，但尚不足以改
變對印尼股市中期前景的保守判斷。
4. 股市跌深不代表利空出盡，中期風險報酬吸引力偏低：印尼股市在 10 月 16 日及 19
日大幅修正後，雖曾連續四個交易日反彈，但 26 日再度回落，顯示市場尚未形成穩
定回升趨勢。我們認為，當前股市面臨的不僅是短期賣壓，更包括國際投資人信任下
降及風險溢價提高，即使企業基本面未同步惡化，仍可能因評價下修而承受股價壓力

。因此，短線不排除政策利多或跌深後出現技術性反彈，但在 MSCI 態度、外資流向
與印尼盾走勢明顯改善前，反彈延續性仍有疑慮，印尼股市中期展望維持保守。
四、市場展望：
1. 印尼股市維持保守展望，單一市場風險報酬吸引力不足：考量 MSCI 降評風險尚未解除、
外資撤離壓力持續，以及市場治理改革仍有待驗證，我們認為印尼股市未來數季的風險報
酬吸引力相對有限。即使近期股價明顯回落，仍難認定相關風險已充分反映。相較市場制
度成熟、國際資金流動性較佳的新興市場，印尼股市可能持續面臨較高風險溢價，因此對
單一印尼市場的集中曝險應維持審慎態度。
2. 此事件對新興市場指數與東協指數影響相對有限：根據 MSCI 資料，截至 2026 年 9 月底
，印尼股市在 MSCI Emerging Market 指數與 MSCI AC ASEAN 指數中的權重分別約為
0.43%與 7.55%，因此我們認為印尼潛在降評對相關指數的直接衝擊相對有限。此外，新
興市場各國經濟結構與產業組成有所差異，區域型基金可透過跨國分散降低單一市場風險

。惟後續仍須留意印尼事件對整體新興市場投資信心造成的外溢影響，以及全球貿易與匯
率波動風險。

鴻海 (2317 TT) 
規模效益顯現

永豐觀點 
AI 伺服器為成長主軸，規模效益顯現，觀察政策長期對 AI 資料中心建置的影響。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持鴻海買進建議：鴻海 3Q26 財報、4Q26 展望優於預期。2027 年維持
以 AI 伺服器為成長主軸，公司表示 2027 年 AI 伺服器產業需求仍大於供給，關鍵
零組件持續吃緊，惟仍需觀察期中選舉後，政策長期對大型企業 AI 資料中心建置
時程的變化。研究部認為鴻海在提高市占率、營收規模放大的同時體現獲利率效
益、獲利金額擴大以及營運資金運用效率。研究部預估鴻海 2026 年/2027 年稅後
EPS 19.33 元/25.51 元。維持鴻海買進建議，目標價 356 元(14x2027FEPS)。

ESG 評析 
鴻海企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。

2

鴻海為專業 ODM/EMS 廠商，產品涵蓋手機、伺服器、平板、網通設備等終端產
品，以及各類電子零組件如連接器、連接線等，旗下重要子公司包含工業富聯
(601138 SH)、富智康(2038 HK)及鴻騰精密(6088 HK)，近年公司開始積極投入研
發資源在電動車、數位醫療、機器人、AI、半導體、5G 等領域。主要客戶為 Apple、
HP、Dell、Sony、Cisco、北美四大 CSP 等。3Q26 產品營收占比：消費暨智能
29%、雲端網路 54%、電腦終端 12%、元件及其他 5%。 
3Q26 財報優於預期：鴻海 3Q26 毛利率 6.02%，營益率 3.83%，優於預估的
5.93%、3.77%。鴻海營收規模放大，營運槓桿效益顯現。業外收入貢獻 33 億元，
稅後淨利 775 億元，稅後 EPS 5.51 元，優於預估 5.10 元。3Q26 鴻海存貨金額
1.5 兆，季增 10%，年增 27%，存貨天數 45.7 天，季減 3 天，年減 4 天。存貨狀
況尚屬健康。 
4Q26 展望略優於預期：鴻海展望 4Q26 營運，AI 伺服器出貨量季增高雙位數(部
分專案採 Consignment 商業模式)，預期 ASIC 及 GPU 出貨續增，Vera Rubin AI
伺服器機櫃單價跳升，推估雲端網路營收季增約 15%。消費性產品 iPhone 維持
強勁出貨動能，PC 需求則持續疲弱季減。研究部略為上修 4Q26 營收預估季增
17%，稅後 EPS 上修至 5.99 元。 
AI 伺服器為鴻海營運增長動能 留意長期資料中心建置影響性：因應客戶 AI 建置
需求，鴻海持續支出投入產能，主要客戶為美系大型 CSP、AI 模型商及 Neocloud，
降低資金及庫存風險。並透過與客戶改成採用 Consignment 商業模式，提高營運
資金效率，並優化獲利率表現。研究部預期鴻海維持 Vera Rubin 的主要供應商角
色，整體市占率維持達 40%以上，同時鴻海 2027 年將增加美系 CSP AI ASIC 伺
服器機櫃供應，為 2027 年營運增長動能。公司表示 2027 年 AI 伺服器產業需求
仍大於供給，關鍵零組件持續吃緊，惟仍需觀察期中選舉後，政策長期對大型企業
AI 資料中心建置時程的變化。研究部認為鴻海在提高市占率、營收規模放大的同
時體現獲利率效益、獲利金額擴大以及營運資金運用效率。 
綜合以上，研究部預估 2026 年營收 11.2 兆，年增 38%，毛利率 6.0%，營益率
3.7%，稅後淨利 2,710 億，年增 43%，稅後 EPS 19.33 元。2027 年營收 15.5 兆
元，年增 38%，毛利率 5.4%，營益率 3.4%，稅後淨利 3,562 億元，年增 31%，
稅後 EPS 25.51 元。

### Page 1

ab 10 November 2026
Global Research
First Read
ChipMOS Technologies
Q326 earnings ahead on better GM; optics and
AI ASIC testing support 2027E growth
Strong margin recovery drives Q326 EPS beat
Q326 EPS came in ahead at NT$1.89 vs. UBSe/street of NT$1.27/1.47 due to higher
margins. Q326 sales was already posted through monthly sales at NT$8.4bn, +14%
QoQ,  supported  by robust  demand from memory (51% of sales, +13% QoQ) on
improving commodity DRAM volume and better momentum from driver IC due to OLED

iPhone ramp  (27% of DDIC sales, +46% QoQ), while it also raised memory backend ASP
to reflect rising component cost. Q326 GM improved to 21.7% (+3.7ppt QoQ) on better
pricing, lower pass through for bumping and higher loading, and  exceeded our estimate
of 18.6%. Q326 OPEX remains well controlled at 6.4% of sales, while it also booked
one-off other operating income of NT$373mn, resulting in better OPM of 19.7%.
Memory and OLED momentum to sustain into Q426

ChipMOS sees better momentum for memory over driver IC business into Q426F  on
continued strength from commodity DRAM and improving NAND Flash outlook. For its
DDIC/bumping business, despite softer end-demand for consumer electronics,
particularly PC and Android smartphone, ChipMOS noted the seasonal build for OLED
iPhone should provide some cushion for its driver IC sales. Management noted it will
continue to optimize its product portfolio and divest lower-end capacity, while

negotiates with its customers on pass through higher component cost if needed. We
forecast ChipMOS's Q426E sales to grow 2% QoQ, with GM expanding by 0.3ppt QoQ
to 22.0% on better  product mix and normalized electricity cost.
Optics and AI ASIC testing support 2027E growth
Management noted its copper bumping for US customers' next-generation products
remain under qualification and expects to see more meaningful revenue contribution by
late-2027F or 2028F. In the meantime, it is  working with the customer on producing

some legacy products in 2027F, although revenue contribution remains limited. As
optics-related business carries significantly better pricing than traditional DDIC
bumping, ChipMOS will prioritize its driver IC/bumping resource for optics and OLED
DDI, instead for Korean customer's outsourcing. Separately, ChipMOS continues to
expand its ASIC testing roadmap, having already entered mass production for
smartphone AP and expecting to progress to smartphone SoC testing, followed by ASIC

final test opportunities if the current program ramps successfully through H127.
Valuation: Reiterate Buy rating with price target of NT$155
We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 6-23% on  improving GM profile driven by more favorable
mix but trim our PT from NT$166 to NT$155 as we apply a lower multiple of 20x 2027-
28E average PE (from 23x) given the recent market valuation contraction. Reiterate Buy
rating on improving margin profiles new AI (optics and logic testing) initiatives.

Company Description
ChipMOS is a semiconductor back-end supplier with 45-50% of its sales from display driver
IC (~40% from TV and 40% from smartphone and IT and 20% from automotive), 35-40%
from memory (~40% DRAM, ~40% from specialty flash and ~20% from mainstream NAND)
and 10-15% from logic (TV SoC) and mixed signal IC (MEMS, PMIC).

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target for ChipMOS is based on a target PE multiple. Downside risks include a
weaker-than-expected tech end-market demand recovery, growing China competition,
failure to diversify into the SiPh/optical component bumping business and a slower ramp of its
logic testing business.

by the analyst to indicate that the stock's price target and/or rating may change in the near term, usually in response to an event that

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

## Document 146: 261111_daiwa_wiwynn

Source PDF: all report pdf/261111_daiwa_wiwynn.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We update our view after 3Q26 results (issued 6 November).

What’s the impact: Fundamentals largely on track. Rising CPU demand 
from agentic AI has lifted our 2026 regular server growth target to >50% 
YoY (vs. 20-30% YoY), making regular servers the key growth driver for 
Wiwynn and helping it offset the slower-than-expected AMZN ASIC AI 
server ramp in 3Q26 (ramping from September vs. original July estimate). 
Looking ahead, we expect the T3 lifecycle to likely peak in 2Q/3Q27, but 
the rising contribution from both Nvidia and AMD GPU AI servers should 
offset the slowdown in 2H27. As for the margin trend, we expect regular 
servers to be at 10% and ASIC AI servers to be at 8%. However, we expect 
the rising GPU AI server to gradually dilute the blended gross margin as 
Wiwynn focuses only on L11, while GPU motherboard (L6) and tray level 
(L10) will be handled by its parent company Wistron (3231 TT, TWD171, 
Buy [1]). As such, we expect the gross margin for Wiwynn’s GPU AI servers 
to be 2-3%, lower than the 4-5% AI of other ODM peers.

3Q26 results beat expectations. Wiwynn reported 3Q26 net income of 
TWD23.36bn (EPS: TWD41.46), beating our expectation by 8%. 3Q26 
sales (TWD434.62bn; up 56% QoQ and 63% YoY) beat our estimate by 6% 
and the Bloomberg consensus estimate by 7% on stronger-than-expected 
regular server shipments. Regular servers accounted for 50-55% of its 
revenue, down from 60-70% in 2Q26, while AI servers contributed 45-50%. 
Gross margin declined by 0.4pp QoQ to 8.9%, but still exceeded our 8.2% 
estimate. Management attributed the sequential margin contraction mainly 
to lower NRE recognition and rising material costs, as not all memory 
components used in regular servers are procured under consignment 
arrangements. However, we believe lower regular server contribution to the 
product mix was key reason. With better operating leverage, operating 
margin improved by 0.2pp QoQ to 7.5%. That said, net profit was dragged 
down by higher interest expense (reaching TWD3bn in 3Q26 vs. TWD2bn 
in 2Q26) and a higher tax rate of 23.2% (vs. 21.1% in 2Q26).

What we recommend: We lift our 2026-27E EPS by 1-5% to reflect 
stronger regular server demand but cut 2028E EPS by 3% to reflect higher 
interest expense assumption. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating but trim our 
12M TP to TWD2,500 (from TWD2,715), based on a target PER of 13x 
(prior: 18x; within its past-2-year trading range of 9-21x) applied to our 1-
year forward diluted EPS, given weaker market sentiment. Key downside 
risks: weaker-than-expected server demand and CSP capex.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 3% above street estimates due to our 
higher regular and AI server shipment assumptions.

11 November 2026

➢ Regular server strength sustaining into 2H26, thanks to agentic AI 
➢ Increasing AI server projects from 4Q26 to boost revenue momentum 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; trimming 12-month TP to TWD2,500

Company profile 
Established in 2012 and listed in 2019, Wiwynn (a subsidiary of Wistron Corp) is a dedicated ODM-
direct supplier of servers for hyperscale datacentres, with major customers including Facebook, 
Microsoft, and Amazon. Aside from hyperscaler customers, the company is expanding its target markets 
to what it terms "Next Wave" customers, including tier-2 cloud service providers and telecom operators, 
which could bring growth opportunities related to edge computing and open RAN.

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Wiwynn plans to have seven to nine directors on its board who will serve three-year terms in
accordance with the Articles of Incorporation in Taiwan. Currently, Wiwynn has 9 board
members with five independent directors. The background of independent directors is diversified
and their competence includes technology, business management, and law. We deem the
diversified background as a positive factor for the company’s board quality. Wiwynn held 9 board
meetings in 2025 and the average attendance rate was around 99%.
The Chairman does not serve concurrently as the President or CEO, which we think is a positive
for board and executive management as well as external shareholder interest.
Capital management 2
Wiwynn declared cash DPS of TWD145 and a stock dividend of TWD20 per share for 2025,
representing a total payout ratio of 60.0%. We note that investors favour its high dividend payout
ratio policy. However, Wiwynn has been expanding its own manufacturing capacity in Malaysia,
Tainan, Mexico and the US, making its business model less asset-light than before. With the
server market still in the high growth period and the payout ratio remaining at around 60%, we
expect the favourable dividend policy to be sustained going forward.
Related party &
transaction 3
In 2025, revenue from related parties accounted for 0.2% of total revenue, while purchases from
related parties were 9.2% of cost of sales. The main purchase counterparty is Wistron (3231 TT;
Wiwynn's parent company), with purchases from Wistron accounting for 8.09% of total
procurement. While Wiwynn is a professional assembly vendor for data center servers, it still
outsources a portion of its production capacity to its parent company, Wistron. At the same time,
Wiwynn has expanded its own production bases in Malaysia, Tainan, Mexico and the US,
reducing its reliance on Wistron. This business model still causes meaningful transactions with
related parties, although the exposure has declined materially. Indeed, we are concerned
whether Wistron collects fair manufacturing fees from Wiwynn. As the company states that
trading terms of purchase transactions with related parties cannot be compared with third-party
vendors due to product specifications, we are not able to assess this concern.
S Supply chain management 1
Wiwynn focuses on providing various products and system solutions for hyperscale datacentres
and cloud infrastructures. There are over 100 suppliers providing key components, electronic
materials, and mechanical materials in Wiwynn’s supply chain. For the new joiners that target to
penetrate into Wiwynn’s supply chain, Wiwynn has formed the “Supplier Evaluation Group” to
conduct: 1) supplier management and supply capabilities evaluation, 2) technical capability
development assessment, and 3) partner quality assurance (these include mainly topics
undertaken by RBA, such as no child labour, no conflict minerals, no use of environmentally
hazardous substances, etc.). New suppliers are also required to be ISO9001, ISO14001, IECQ
QC 080000, and OHSAS 18001 (ISO 45001) qualified.
Wiwynn has strong supply chain risk management by conducting audits regularly. Apart from
considering the technical skills, quality date, delivery dates and price completeness of suppliers,
Wiwynn audits its suppliers to endeavour to protect the environment, ensure safety and health of
the environment, and emphasise the importance of human rights, based on Wiwynn’s CSR
Management System.
S Data security 2
To serve world-class cloud service providers, Wiwynn has been constantly improving its
information security management. Employees are educated on the protection of the product,
technology, and information privacy of customers.
In response to the global trends in data protection laws that tend to strengthen the security of
classified information, Wiwynn has mandated that all assigned PCs have the Windows Bitlocker
HDD encryption activated to avoid classified data being accessed without permission. In 2020, it
also introduced Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA). Beyond the security measures for hardware,
the company controls access to office, labs, and conference room in a robust manner to prevent
theft of the hardware.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
4Q NB build forecast cut 5% despite October’s beat: October ODM NB shipments
came in 4% above MSe, but we lower our 4Q26 build forecast by 5% to 21.9M units
(-13% q/q, -32% y/y) to reflect the latest ODM guidance. The revised forecast implies

limited quarter-end pull-in strength and a deeper y/y contraction than the 26%
decline in 3Q26. Despite an already depressed 3Q base, the projected 13%
sequential decline is well below the 15-year average of flat q/q shipments in 4Q.
PC outlook seen continuing to weaken into 2027: NB build trends have weakened
over recent months, while increasingly cautious ODM commentary provides further
evidence of pressure on PC demand. Asustek expects global PC unit shipments to

decline 10–15% y/y in 2027 (link), while Quanta expects global NB shipments to
drop by high single digits to 10% y/y (link). We believe 2027 outlooks from the
supply chain have worsened in recent months, and both outlooks are directionally
consistent with our forecast for an 11.5% y/y decline in global PC units in 2027.
Looking to 1Q27, our preliminary view is a further sequential decline in NB builds,
although we have yet to establish a formal forecast.

## Document 186: 261116_gs_aspeed

Source PDF: all report pdf/261116_gs_aspeed.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-16

Allen Chang 
+852-2978-2930 | 
allen.k.chang@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C. 
Verena Jeng 
+852-2978-1681 | verena.jeng@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
Aspeed (5274.TWO): Mgmt. visit: BMC and SMC specification migration 
and customers’ expansion to drive growth ahead; Buy
16 November 2026 | 11:05PM HKT
We visited Aspeed in Taipei recently. Management remain positive on future growth, 
riding on further customer expansion, product mix upgrade, and their continuous 
new product launches. The company highlights its comprehensive coverage across 
various chipset platforms (e.g. GPU, ASIC, RISC-V, etc.) and coverage across different 
layers in AI data center (e.g. component-level, server-level, rack-level, data 
center-level), securing their leading market position. We remain positive on Aspeed 
and see the growing servers demand along with AI servers design getting more 
complicated to support the company’s growth ahead. Maintain Buy. Read more: 
Aspeed Oct revenues and 3Q26 results in line. 
Key takeaways 
1. Positive tone on future growth: Management remain positive on future business 
growth, driven by further customer expansion, product mix upgrade, and their 
continuous new product launches. The company’s shipment is mainly from BMC2600 
in 2026, and 1700 and 2700 would further expand shipment in 2027, carrying higher 
ASP as stronger specification (e.g. integrated more functions, I/O supporting DDR5). 
The company would also have 2705 prior to 2800, which offers stronger security. The 
2800 is targeted to start sampling in 2028, and would be a driver beyond 2028, 
integrating more functions, stronger security (e.g. silicon-level security), stronger I/O, 
and using more advanced processing nodes. On SMC and Platform, the company will 
also have new products entering mass production in 2H27: (1) SMC 1040 and 1840 
(embedded FPGA, serving both single-node and multi-node), and (2) platform 1080, 
aiming to further expand customers. The company’s pure exposure to the server 
market also brings it strong end market demand, with the ability to pass through the 
higher raw material costs to end customers (e.g. higher costs from substrate, OSAT, 
foundry), supporting its GM. 
2. Competition advantages: Management highlight they are early in the market and 
serve various customers (e.g. GPU, ASIC, RISC-V, etc.), with comprehensive product 
coverage across different SoC platforms, and the company is also expanding its 
product portfolio vertically: across SMC (component-level mgmt.), BMC 
(server-level), and RMC (rack-level). The company’s products can monitor various 
scenarios, such as GPU thermal issue, liquid cooling leakage, etc. Management also 
highlight their large R&D team (over 100 engineers), local and timely support (having 
R&D team in Taiwan, close to customers), and ecosystem (work closely with ODMs). 
We remain positive on Aspeed’s leading market position and see AI server 
specifications getting more and more complicated (e.g. more shelves, more chips per

### Page 2

rack, from air cooling to liquid cooling, etc.), which would further enhance Aspeed’s 
competition edges. 
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Aspeed (5274.TWO) 
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$22,000 is based on a target P/E of 40x (in line with 
its 10-year historical trading average) applied to our 2028E EPS, and discounted back to 
2027E at 13.6% CoE. Our key assumptions for CoE include: (1) 1.5x beta (sourcing 
Bloomberg), (2) a 4.25% risk-free rate, and (3) a market risk premium at 6.25% (in line 
with GS house view). 
Key downside risks to our view and TP: 1) A softer-than-expected server market 
demand recovery, 2) slower-than-expected BMC penetration within AI servers, 3) 
intensifying market competition.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 16 Nov 2026 close.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

4576 大銀微(HMC)

報告日期：2026/11/12 研 究 員：洪 國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
 在半導體先進封裝、AI 及矽光子等高階應用需求暢旺，其中
微米與奈米級定位系統交期 5~6 個月，精密運動及控制元件交
期約 3~4 個月，呈現供不應求。 
 受惠於晶圓大廠持續擴充先進製程、後段設備廠訂單持續成
長，大銀微在超高精度平台訂單已經看到 2027 年年中，另外
搭配新產能將於 2Q27 投產。預估 2027 年微米與奈米級定位
系統營收金額 31.5 億元，YoY+28.0%。 
一. 公告 3Q26 稅後 EPS 2.14 元 
大銀微 3Q26 營收金額 12.18 億元，YoY+76.73%，QoQ+19.01%，
營業利益 2.96 億元，YoY+312.6%，QoQ+61.12%，稅後盈餘 2.57
億元，YoY+255.8%，QoQ+123.72%，稅後 EPS 2.14 元。 
二. 預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 2.44 元 
大銀微 10/2026 營收金額 4.10 億元，YoY+68.75%，MoM-3.96%。
在訂單呈現滿載水準，預估大銀微 4Q26 營收金額 12.84 億元，
YoY+69.94% ， QoQ+5.37% ，預估營業利益 3.32 億元，
YoY+232.13% ， QoQ+12.21% ，預估稅後盈餘 2.92 億元，
YoY+223.25%，QoQ+13.78%，預估稅後 EPS 2.44 元。 
三. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 6.50 元 
大銀微 2026 年在半導體、PCB 與矽光子檢測裝置需求延續，帶
動微米奈米級高精度定位系統訂單維持數個月的能見度。另外中
國大陸工具機谷底復甦，推升控制元件產品需求，帶動大銀微 2026
年營運成長。預估大銀微 2026 年營收 43.8 億元，YoY+61.37%，
預估稅後盈餘 7.79 億元，YoY+222.63%，預估稅後 EPS 6.50 元。 
四. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 9.00 元 
大銀微 2027 年在推動的 One HIWIN Solution 單一零組件供應
商，往次系統、完整定位平台以及共同開發解決方案的角色移動，
搭配同集團上銀資源整合，將有助於大銀微產品附加價值提升。
宏遠預估大銀微 2027 年營收金額 53.00 億元，YoY+21.00%，預
估稅後盈餘 10.79 億元，YoY+38.52%，預估稅後 EPS 9.00 元。 
五. 投資建議買進，目標價 252 元 
觀察國內傳動元件同業 2027 年營收成長率亞德客 ROE 21%，營
收成長率 10%，PER 15X ；上銀 ROE 11% ，營收成長率 16%，
PER 22X，大銀微 ROE 21%，營收成長率 21%，PER 21.6X，以
ROE 與營收成長率相較同業來的佳，評價與同業相較仍在低檔水
準，故投資建議買進，目標價 252 元（28X 2027 年稅後 EPS）

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
大銀微系統股份有限公司創立於 1997 年 4 月，資本額 11.98 億
元，並於 2019 年 9 月正式掛牌上市。大銀微行屬於上銀集團旗
下的核心關係企業。大銀微聚焦精準定位與高性能驅動控制技
術，並以機電整合、產品製造及技術服務之整體能力作為核心競
爭優勢，深耕半導體、電子生產設備、智慧自動化及工具機等領
域。受惠半導體先進製程與封裝需求成長，大銀微以領先業界之
精密定位技術及系統整合方案擴大服務範圍，針對客戶需求提供
專業精密定位系統整合方案，透過技術互補與研發合作，協助全 
球頂尖半導體客戶縮短開發週期並提升產出效益。

二. 2026 年營運概況及 2027 年展望 
大銀行營收主要有精密運動及控制元件、微米與奈米級定位系統
與其他三部分。

預估 2027 年精密運動及控制元件營收金額 21.00 億元，
YoY+11.7%

精密運動及控制元件產品線包括：線性馬達、直驅馬達、真空馬
達、力矩馬達、伺服馬達、高速主軸馬達、位置量測系統等，並
搭配自行開發的驅動器及控制器，達奈米級高精度定位控制功
能。廣泛應用於半導體設備/自動化搬送/FPD 設備/PCB 設備
/AOI 檢測設備/SMT 設備/雷射加工器/各式機器手臂/工具機/電
動車相關/太陽能等不同產業。精密運動及控制元件 2025 年營收
金額 12.64 億元，YoY+3.1%。

精密運動及控制元件 1Q~3Q26 營收金額 13.83 億元，
YoY+45.43%。在中國大陸對驅動器需求高，目前處於供不應求狀
態，帶動包括線性馬達、直驅馬達、伺服馬達產能稼動率在高檔
水準，預估 2026 年精密運動及控制元件產品營收金額 18.80 億
元，YoY+48.7%。

預期 2027 年，在中國大陸工具機出口額與金屬切削設備產業仍呈
現正向發展，整體需求大於供給，訂單能見度己達 3~4 個月，預
估 2027 年精密運動及控制元件產品營收金額 21.00 億元，
YoY+11.7%。

預估 2027 年微米與奈米級定位系統營收金額 31.5 億元，
YoY+28.0%

微米與奈米級定位系統產品線包括：各式高精密定位平台，如單
軸、X-Y 雙軸、橋式、龍門、空氣軸承、多維度定位平台，可滿
足各種產業應用的需求，如自動化設備、汽車產業、光學檢測、
雷射加工、膠材塗佈、金屬加工等。微米與奈米級定位系統 2025
年營收金額 14.27 億元，YoY+41.8%。

微米與奈米級定位系統 1Q~3Q26 營收金額 16.8 億元，
YoY+69.5%。在 AI 產業熱潮帶動半導體產業投資本支出持續加
速，間接推升微米與奈米級定位系統需求，其中半導體定位平台
能見度約 5~6 個月，超高精度平台甚至可看到 2027 年中，在此
熱潮帶動下，預估 2026 年微米與奈米級定位系統營收金額 24.60
億元，YoY+72.4%。

預期 2027 年，受惠於晶圓大廠持續擴充先進製程、後段設備廠訂
單持續成長，大銀微在超高精度平台訂單已經看到 2027 年年中，
包括 CoWoP、CoPoS、FoPLP 都持續配合客戶打樣中，另外搭
配新產能將於 2Q27 投 產。預 估 2027 年微米與奈米級定位系統營
收金額 31.5 億元，YoY+28.0%。

## Document 218: [000270] 기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다잠시 쉬어갑니다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000270] 기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다잠시 쉬어갑니다 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.11.02  
잠시 쉬어갑니다

3Q26 Review 
기아는 3Q26P 매출액 31.6조원(+10.1% YoY, -4.4% QoQ), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.4% YoY, -30.8% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회했다. 영업이익 증
감 요인은 환율 효과(+5,290억원), 물량 효과(+4,420억원), 믹스 효과(-4,650억원), 
인센티브/가격 효과(-7,270억원), 재료비 영향(-3,680억원), 기타(+4,130억원)이다. 
판매 성장에도 높아진 수익성 부담 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.9% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.1만대
(+8.8%)를 기록했다. 미국 HEV와 서유럽 EV 판매가 각각 118%, 163% 증가하며 
글로벌 점유율은 4.0%까지 확대됐다. 다만, 판매 성장에도 원화 강세와 원자재 가격 
상승, EV 비중 확대에 따른 믹스 부담으로 수익성은 전분기 대비 하락했다. 유럽 내 
EV 판매가 늘어나면서 믹스가 악화되었으며, 전쟁으로 인한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 
재료비 부담은 전년 대비 약 3,700억 증가했다.  
연간 판매목표 335만대는 유지했으나, 4분기 수익성의 급격한 반등은 어려울 전망이
다. 향후 미국 텔루라이드·스포티지 HEV 공급 확대와 유럽 EV 판매 증가에 따른 고정
비 흡수가 수익성 회복의 핵심이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 155,000원으로 하향 
기아에 대한 투자의견 Buy를 유지하고, 실적 추정치 하향을 반영해 목표주가를 
155,000원으로 하향한다. 재료비 부담, 환율 효과 축소, 예상보다 큰 믹스 악화로 단
기 이익 눈높이 조정은 불가피하다. 다만 산업수요 둔화에도 주요 시장에서 점유율을 
확대되고 있다는 점은 긍정적이다. 현재 배당 수익률은 7% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높
은 배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

목표주가 (하향) 155,000 원 
현재주가 95,600 원 
상승여력 62.1 %

## Document 238: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가현저한 저평가 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가현저한 저평가 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

00

삼성전자 (005930/KS) 
현저한 저평가 
3Q26 영업이익 106 조원 예상 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익을 106조원 (+18% QoQ)으로 예상한다. 이는 기존 당사 
전망치 111 조원 대비 4.5% 하향된 것이다. 원/달러 환율 가정을 기존 1,450 원에서 
1,400 원으로 3.4% 하향, MX/NW 사업부의 적자 폭을 확대 반영 (-0.5 조원 -> -
1.4 조원)했기 때문이다. 주가의 핵심인 메모리 판가는 DRAM +17%, NAND +15%
로 오히려 예상을 소폭 상회할 것으로 예상된다. 출하는 수요 강세에도 불구하고 재고 
부족, HBM 확대에 따른 제한적 공급 여력으로 DRAM +4%, NAND +7%의 기존 예
상을 유지한다. HBM 은 분기 최대 출하를 경신하기 시작할 것이다. 또한 AI 강세로 파
운드리 수주 및 가동률의 동반 상승이 지속 중이다. 성과 충당금 반영 등에 따라 단기 
흑자전환 가시성은 제한적이나, DS 사업부 전체적으로 강화되고 있는 국면이다.

2027 년 568 조원 (+55% YoY) 전망 유지. 환율 하락 반영 
SK증권은 삼성전자의 2027년 영업이익 전망치를 568조원으로 유지한다. 원/달러 환
율 가정을 기존 1,440 원에서 1,340 원으로 7% 하향했음에도, 실적 전망을 유지하는 
이유는 AI 메모리 수요 강세 속 공급 부족 심화, LTA에 따른 Dual market 효과에 의
한 Commodity 메모리 가격 강세, 2027년 HBM 판가 강세 (+103% YoY) 와 출하 
강세, 수익성 회복의 동반, 파운드리 적자 폭 축소의 본격화 전망 때문이다. 2027년 메
모리 출하는 DRAM +22%, NAND +16%, 판가는 DRAM +38%, NAND +28%
를 예상한다. 시장에서 우려하는 메모리 가격 하락, 감익 시나리오는 없을 것이다.

차별적 실적과 주주환원 본격화. 현저한 저평가 
SK증권은 메모리 업황과 삼성전자의 주가가 현저히 저평가 되어있다고 판단한다. 삼성
전자의 2027E P/E는 4.2X에 불과하며, 이익 창출력은 글로벌 최상위권이다. 주주환
원 정책 또한 기존의 FCF 50% 환원이 향후에도 유지될 것으로 가정한다면, 2027년 
193 조원, 2028 년 222 조원으로 현재 시가총액 (우선주 포함) 대비 각각 11%, 13% 
수준에 육박한다. 성장을 위한 투자를 확대하고 있음에도, 대규모의 환원이 가능하다. 
LTA는 AI 시대 메모리 수요의 질과 실적 가시성을 제고시키며, 이는 주주환원의 지속
가능성을 의미한다. 메모리 가격의 높은 기저, LTA 물량의 계약 가격 적용을 감안하면, 
가격 상승률 자체는 안정화될 것이다. 다만 실적 안정성, 가시성, Duration을 확보하기 
시작했다는 점이 훨씬 중요한 가치이며, 아직 시장의 Playbook에 없을 뿐이다. 시대는 
변하고 있고, 시장도 변해야 한다. 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 610,000원을 유지한다. 
2026-10-06

반도체. 한동희 / donghee.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-8826

본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다. 
투자판단 3 단계(6 개월기준) 15%이상 → 매수 / -15%~15% → 중립 / -15%미만 → 매도

## Document 282: Altman：AI行业将安然度过黄仁勋出局风波

Source PDF: all report pdf/Altman：AI行业将安然度过黄仁勋出局风波.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
⻩仁勋因婚外情丑闻离职后， OpenAI CEO Sam Altman 公开表态称 AI ⾏业 “ 将会安然⽆
恙 ” ，理由是营收与需求增⻓依然强劲。他拒绝就⻩仁勋是否应复职表态，但援引⾃⼰三年
前被解雇⼜迅速复职的经历，表达了对⻩仁勋处境的个⼈共情。
摘要：
英伟达 CEO ⻩仁勋的突然离职令 AI 市场陷⼊不确定性，但 OpenAI 掌⻔⼈ Sam Altman 给出了⼀
个相对平稳的判断。
Altman 11 ⽉ 16 ⽇周⼀在旧⾦⼭举⾏的 Semafor“ 硅⾕与世界 ” 峰会上表⽰，AI ⾏业的营收与需求
增⻓势头依然强劲，⾜以抵御这场⼈事震荡带来的冲击。
不过，他也承认⻩仁勋的离去并⾮⽆⾜轻重。 Altman 补充道， “ 我认为⻩仁勋扮演了⼀个极其特
殊且宝贵的⻆⾊，他是真正以远⻅卓识推动事情向前发展的⼈。 ”

在 Altman 看来，即便⻩仁勋缺席，英伟达作为⾏业基础设施提供商的地位短期内不会动摇。
拒绝表态复职问题，但共情溢于⾔表
对于外界最关⼼的问题 —— ⻩仁勋是否应当复职 ——Altman 选择了回避。
与此同时，他援引⾃⼰三年前被解雇⼜迅速复职的亲⾝经历，对⻩仁勋的处境表达了个⼈共
情。他说，⾃⼰对 “ 这样的处境有强烈的个⼈共情 ”—— 这显然是在指向近三年前他被 OpenAI 董
事会解雇⼀事。彼时， Altman 在不到五天内便重返 CEO 职位，原因是公司⼤多数员⼯威胁要随
他⼀同离开。
围绕⻩仁勋是否应当复职，硅⾕正在形成⼀股强⼤的声援⼒量。据英国《⾦融时报》报道，⻩
仁勋本⼈已在讨论回归的可能性， Altimeter Capital 、 Atreides Management 以及特斯拉 CEO ⻢
斯克均已向英伟达董事会施压，要求重新考虑这⼀决定。特朗普周⼀也公开表态，⽀持⻩仁勋
复职。
营收和需求正在如此强劲地增⻓，我认为这个⾏业将会安然⽆恙。
英伟达是⼀家庞然⼤物，我认为⼈们仍然⾮常信任他们的承诺。

我认为我没有⾜够的了解来回答这个问题。⻩仁勋⻓期以来是我⼼⽬中的英雄；英雄也有缺点。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

Economics | 3

### Page 4

Monthly Economics
Sector Analysis
Personal Consumption Expenditures (PCE)
• We did not materially alter our real PCE forecast, and expect consumer spending growth to
continue.
Consumer spending has held up remarkably well this year despite higher gasoline costs weighing on
household purchasing power. While we do not expect the recent above-trend near-4% annualized
pace of real consumption growth to be sustained, we see consumers continuing to spend at a healthy

clip into next year. A firmer income backdrop revealed by recent revisions, continued support from
household wealth and an expected recovery in purchasing power as inflation slows should keep
consumption on solid footing. We expect real consumption growth to remain north of 2% in the final
quarter of the year and roughly match this year’s average pace in 2027.
Investment: Equipment, Intellectual Property Products, and Inventories
• A stronger-than-expected capex outturn in Q3 has revealed a slightly firmer trend headed into

next year.
Business investment continues to be driven by the high-tech buildout, with real equipment spending
stronger than expected in Q3 and the latest data suggest a firmer trend. We have not materially
changed our outlook following the Democratic sweep in the midterms. Our forecast already assumes
some moderation in high-tech investment next year and a smaller contribution to GDP growth as the
buildout matures. Beyond high-tech, we expect only a modest broadening in capex spending over the

coming year.
Source: U.S. Department of Commerce and Wells Fargo Economics
Source: U.S. Department of Commerce and Wells Fargo Economics
Investment: Residential
• We have not made major changes to our outlook for residential investment. High mortgage rates
and challenging affordability conditions look set to continue pressuring home sales and new single-
family construction.
Not much has changed in the way of residential investment. Mortgage rates have come in somewhat,

but are still elevated above 7.0%. Building permits are stuck in a low gear, with single-family projects
continuing to moderate against a backdrop of weak buyer demand and elevated inventories.
Multifamily permits have picked up modestly, reflecting renewed strength in apartment demand.
Modestly lower mortgage rates and stabilizing apartment market conditions should help put a floor
beneath residential investment and bring about modest growth over the course of next year.

Investment: Nonresidential Structures
• Structures investment is likely to maintain healthy momentum amid robust data center, power, and
related nonresidential construction.
4 | Economics

### Page 5

Economic Outlook: November 2026 Economics
Structures investment accelerated notably in Q3. The bulk of this momentum can be traced to data
centers and power, however, activity also appears to be broadening out. As examples, private spending
on communication and transportation infrastructure have perked up and manufacturing construction
appears to be stabilizing on increased semiconductor investment. The downturn in commercial
construction has also softened as data centers shore up demand for warehousing. Although new

regulations may soften the pace of data center starts, there are more than enough projects already in
the pipeline to support healthy structures investment through next year.

Net Exports & Trade
• Net exports should remain a fairly persistent drag on U.S. real GDP growth amid continued
imports.
A surge in imports, particularly of high-tech goods like computers, caused net exports to subtract

more than 3 percentage points from Q3 GDP growth. We expect trade to remain a drag in Q4, though
with little data in hand, that largely reflects where trade flows ended last quarter and an assumption
of another sizable gain in imports. Looking ahead, continued strength in high-tech investment should
keep import growth elevated and the trade deficit wide, leaving net exports a persistent drag on U.S.
growth. Exports remain more volatile, but imports are increasingly the bigger driver of the trade story.

Labor Market
• Labor demand has improved modestly in recent months. We now expect payroll growth to average
65K per month in 2027, and the unemployment rate to hold steady at 4.1% over the next four
quarters.
The October jobs report reinforced our view that the labor market remains solid. The pickup in job
growth this year aligns with purchasing manager surveys that point to expanding employment across
the goods and services sectors. At the same time, the historically low trends in initial jobless claims and

the layoff rate suggest employers broadly remain reluctant to shed workers. Structurally, firms appear
focused on retaining existing staff and investing heavily in AI adoption while remaining selective about
adding to head count—a dynamic that should keep the low-hire, low-fire environment largely intact.
We expect payroll growth to average around 65K per month in 2027, roughly in line with our estimate
of breakeven job growth. That should keep the unemployment rate steady at 4.1% through next year

and labor market conditions generally consistent with full employment.
Inflation
• We look for the core PCE deflator to rise 3.3% on a Q4-over-Q4 basis in 2026 before slowing to
2.6% in 2027.
The October inflation report reinforced the persistence of price pressures, with upside surprises
emerging across a range of categories. Although gasoline and diesel prices have receded in recent
Economics | 5

### Page 6

Monthly Economics
weeks, earlier increases in transportation costs are still working their way through supply chains. Food
inflation picked up in October and looks poised to strengthen further. The outlook for energy prices
remains highly uncertain and represents the largest swing factor for inflation, in our view. Within the
core, we continue to expect goods inflation to moderate in 2027 as tariff-related pressures fade
further and demand tied to the AI buildout cools. Services inflation, however, is likely to prove stickier.

Health care services prices have been running hot and should continue to face upward pressure from
the aging population, and primary shelter inflation looks to be stabilizing. Taken together, we expect
inflation to remain above the Fed's 2% target through next year and look for the core PCE deflator to
end 2027 at 2.6% on a Q4-over-Q4 basis.

Fiscal Policy
• The lame duck session of Congress probably will not yield a budget reconciliation bill or a debt

ceiling resolution. The December 11 government funding deadline probably will be punted to Q1/
early Q2 via another continuing resolution.
• We project a federal budget deficit of $2.0 trillion for FY 2027, similar to FY 2026 in dollar terms
but a bit smaller as a share of GDP (~6%).
Assuming that Congress does not increase or suspend the debt ceiling in the next six weeks, the
incoming Democratic Congress will need to negotiate a resolution with President Trump in 2027.

Right now, it looks like Treasury will hit the debt ceiling around early March. At that point, Treasury can
lean on extraordinary measures and its cash on hand to last for about 7-8 months, putting the debt
ceiling "X date" somewhere around early Q4. We suspect the debt ceiling/annual budget negotiations
will include a lift to defense and nondefense discretionary government spending for FY 2027/2028,
but we doubt the bump will be enough to meaningfully move our economic growth or deficit forecasts.

For now, our working assumption is that divided government will not produce any major new fiscal
expansions/contractions over the next two years, leaving the budget deficit picture as status quo until
after the 2028 election.
Monetary Policy & Interest Rates
• Our baseline forecast is for another 25 bp rate hike to the fed funds rate in December, followed by
an extended hold from the FOMC through 2027. The risks are tilted toward 1-2 more hikes early

next year.
• Our forecast for the 10-year Treasury yield at year-end 2027 is 4.85%.
The recent inflation data seem to make a hike in December a lock so long as financial conditions don't
completely implode in the coming weeks. The core CPI came in about 8 bps above consensus at 0.3%
for October, and a 0.4% on core PCE is plausible, depending on how the PPI data shake out. This should
put the FOMC's hawks back in the driver's seat after the October skip.

6 | Economics

### Page 7

Economic Outlook: November 2026 Economics
Past December, the outlook will be highly dependent on future inflation data. Our expectation is that
core inflation will cool gradually in 2027, and we still do not see clear signs of demand-side inflation in
the data. But hiking cycles have an inertial component to them, and the recent inflation data reset the
clock to zero on the Fed seeing several good inflation prints in a row. This creates some modest upside

risk to our base case for the fed funds rate next year.

International Developments
• Global growth has proven more resilient, with our 2026 growth forecast revised higher and a
modest improvement in the underlying 2027 outlook.
• Our 2026 inflation forecast has moved slightly lower, while our 2027 forecast has moved higher,
with inflationary risks likely to persist into 2027.
• The monetary policy bias remains toward extended tightening across advanced economies.

Emerging market policy paths remain differentiated, but have also shifted in a more hawkish
direction, with some central banks bringing forward rate hikes and others having less room to ease
in 2027.
The balance of risks to global inflation remains tilted to the upside. Elevated geopolitical uncertainty,
resilient activity and persistent energy and supply-chain pressures increase the risk that inflation
remains higher for longer. Although the direct effects of supply shocks may prove temporary, central

banks are increasingly focused on delayed pass-through, persistent services inflation and potential
second-round effects on wages, prices and inflation expectations. Higher government bond yields are
meanwhile keeping financial conditions tight. The result is a shift toward more extended tightening,
with several rate hikes moving further into the forecast horizon and less room for easing in 2027.
Across advanced economies, the tightening cycle is increasingly extending into 2027. Our European

Central Bank outlook is unchanged, with a 25 bps hike in December still our base case, although
tighter financial conditions raise the risk of a later move. In the U.K., our November Bank of England
hike was realized, and we now expect another 25 bps hike in Q1-2027, reflecting renewed inflation
pressures associated with the Ofgem price-cap reset.
Elsewhere, the policy outlook has turned more hawkish in Canada. Having previously flagged upside
risks to our year-end call, we now expect the Bank of Canada to hike at consecutive meetings, bringing

the policy rate to 3.00% by Q1-2027. In Australia, the Reserve Bank of Australia raised the Cash Rate
again in November. After five hikes, we think policymakers have room to assess how tighter policy
feeds through the economy, although risks remain skewed toward another increase if inflation stays
elevated or activity proves stronger than expected. Japan is unchanged: we continue to expect the
Bank of Japan to hike in December and again in 2027, bringing the terminal rate to 1.75%.

Emerging market policy paths remain differentiated but have also shifted more hawkishly. In India,
resilient growth and rising inflation have led us to bring forward our second Reserve Bank of India
Economics | 7

### Page 8

Monthly Economics
hike from Q1-2027 to December 2026. Brazil is moving toward higher-for-longer policy, with
persistent inflation raising the bar for additional easing; we now expect cuts to resume in Q2 rather
than Q1-2027 and see less easing overall next year. In Mexico, Banxico's cut to 6.25% was more easing
than we expected, and with inflation risks still tilted to the upside, we now expect rates to remain on
hold. China remains the exception: continued targeted policy support and resilient exports should

support growth, prompting most upward revisions to our 2026 and 2027 growth forecasts, while
greater scope for easing likely emerges in 2027.

► 近一週美股、日股及新興市場表現遜於港股
及歐股，因市場擔憂民主黨全面掌控國會恐
影響AI基礎建設擴張進程，令股市承壓，即
便總統川普表態接受共和黨敗選結果並願尋
求民 主 黨 的跨 黨 合 作，仍 無 法緩 解 市 場擔
憂，美股主要指數普遍收跌；歐股方面，面
對全球科技股評價修正，部分資金轉入歐洲
市場尋求避險，使歐股近期呈現跌深反彈；

亞股方面，日股因整體AI供應鏈與美國高度
連結而同步下跌，港股則是受惠騰訊發布新
AI模型帶動相關版塊大漲。

礎建設受限及資本開支見頂、負自由現金流

技與 通 訊 服務 股 大 幅流 出 ，並轉 進 必 需消
費、醫療保健、公用事業等防禦性產業。

熱門議題
► 黃金價格歷經第二季修整，近日呈現大幅反
彈走勢，主要係因美國期中選舉後，市場憂
心AI產業監管將趨嚴，加上晶片與科技股遭
遇大幅修正，促使部分資金轉往黃金等防禦
型資產尋求避險。
► 根據世界黃金協會最新報告，第3季全球央行
淨買進321公噸黃金，較第二季明顯回升，年
增率達42%，創下自2024年第四季以來的單
季新高，前3季累計買進金額更是高達675公
噸，顯示黃金儲備已經列入全球央行必需資
產配置而且比重維持穩定增長態勢。
► 長期以來黃金具有抗通膨特性，觀察黃金與
聯邦基金利率走勢，從2020年之後，即便全
球歷經2次升息循環，黃金價格仍呈現穩健上
揚走勢，反應其抗通膨保值特性。此外，美
國財政赤字壓力難消，恐令美元長期購買力
被稀釋， 加 速 全 球 央 行 去 美 元 化 ， 有 鑑 於
此，建議黃金納入資產配置。

資料來源：Bloomberg，Factset，凱基證券整理；註：右上產業回報相關性為各產業過去3年之每週報酬相關性
► 醫療類股於美國第三季財報展現強勁的獲利
韌性，實際獲利優於預期程度為主要產業中
表現最佳，背後主要受惠大型製藥企業(如：
禮來、再生元製藥、吉利德科學)在減重藥、
癌症治療及創新藥領域之需求持續增長，帶
動整體產業獲利表現優於市場預期。
► 期中選舉後醫療政策輪廓逐漸明朗。民主黨
主張支持擴大醫療保險覆蓋、延續平價醫療
法案補貼以及緩和醫療補助計劃(Medicaid)
削減，有助提升醫療服務使用率；雖藥價談
判政策恐帶來部分定價壓力，但整體政策主
張仍對創新藥、生技研發及醫療保險產業具
潛在政策支持效果。
► 綜上，在AI投資回報率面臨市場檢驗之際，
市場資金持續尋求防禦性配置。醫療保健類
股與資訊科技類股相關性偏低，有助分散投
資組合對單一AI題材依賴，加上第三季獲利
動能轉強、創新藥需求穩健及產業能見度提
升，後續有望持續受惠資金輪動效應。

財報與政策題材雙利多，醫療類股成市場新寵兒

政策議題 民主黨傾向 共和黨傾向 兩黨共識對製藥產業潛在影響
降低藥價 依《通膨削減法案》框架，擴大醫療 補助計劃(Medicaid)藥價談判適用範圍 傾向透過最惠國定價機制來降低藥 價 兩黨均主張降低藥價 製藥企業面臨定價壓力
美國本土製造 透過研發補助、稅收優惠及產業政策 鼓勵藥廠回流美國生產 透過關稅、供應鏈重組及企業承諾 推動製藥產能回流 兩黨均支持醫藥供應鏈自主化 有利本土藥廠及原料藥供應鏈投資
醫療保險覆蓋
支持延長《平價醫療法案》補貼、擴
大醫療補助計劃(Medicaid)覆蓋範圍，
並緩和該補助計劃的削減
傾向由州政府及市場機制主導醫療
體系，限制聯邦政府支出
醫療的可負擔性為兩黨政策共同焦點，有助
增加就醫人口及處方藥使用量，支撐製藥、
醫療服務及保險業長期需求
FDA審查資源 支持增加FDA審查預算與人力配置 著重監管效率優化，額外資源投入 相對有限 兩黨均支持提升新藥審查效率 利多創新藥與生技公司

投資焦點必需消費
► 短期而言，檢視各類股在最近股災表現，美
國成長股、資訊科技、與通訊服務等高波動
度類股，表現明顯落後大盤，其中，資訊科
技與通訊服務波動度最大、跌幅最重，主要
係因美股科技巨頭財報不如預期、負自由現
金流量，疊加期中選舉後民主黨恐緊縮AI基
礎建設進程及資本支出見頂等不確定因素，
令市場 擔憂 導致 資 金流出 ；並轉 進核 心消
費、健康護理、防禦股、公用事業等防禦性
產業，報酬相對較穩健，受市場資金青睞。
► 長期而言，從2008迄今，核心消費類股長期
表現穩健上漲。與主要指數相比，面對近月
AI科技股修正，同期間費半大跌18%、那斯
達克跌幅6%、標普跌幅1%，而核心消費類
股大漲13%，反應其低波動、抗震韌性。
► 整體而言，從2015年至今，歷經2次通膨與
升息循環，核心消費受影響性低，有鑑於資
產配置角度，建議短期跟隨資金流向配置核
心消費股，提高投組抗震韌性。
必需消費長期表現穩健、抗通膨、低波動避險資金流入，必需消費類股單月表現最佳

配置必需消費類股，提高投組抗震韌性

股市
 隨著美國期中選舉結束，美伊衝突可望降溫，市場關注焦點將回歸基本面，惟AI資料中心建設進程
或將受到州政府緊縮政策而造成評價面壓力增添股價波動。短期可布局防禦股、分散配置於不同國
家與產業，增強抗震力、降低市場波動影響，長期而言，AI從全面看好變成要追求財務健全度較高
的品質股、且大優於小，震盪之後長期正向趨勢不變。
 歐、日股可分散配置，利率走升有利銀行股。此外，隨財政政策支持，歐洲國防軍工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，AI從
全面看好變成要追求財務健全度較高的品
質股、且大優於小，震盪之後長期正向趨
勢不變，非AI部分可關注必需消費產業、
醫療保健產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 隨資金湧入，公債殖利率回落，惟根據最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升
息空間，立場偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展。建議以中短天期債券投資為主，可逢利率走升
時布局進行鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引

力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 聯準會12月份會議升息態度偏鷹，美元高檔整理。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期偏鷹，日圓弱勢盤整。

 去美元化支撐全球央行買盤，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
低接買盤流入，黃金低點反彈，中長期維
持偏多看法

### Page 16

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

Tactical plays within a diversified allocation 
Strategy summary: Our calls to dial down risk since early 
October have helped mitigate a bear market in semiconductors 
and the sharpest single-day post-mid-term election downturn in 
US equities in over three decades. Volatility has eased after the 
post-election spike, helping the IT sector partly recover. We see 
further upside in our opportunistic US IT and healthcare sector 
equity ideas. In bonds, we lock in gains on US inflation-
protected bonds as inflation nears a peak.

mid-cycle market dynamics. Last week, as the US equity 
market volatility index VIX surged above 25, we partially dialled 
up risk with a tactical idea on the more diversified US IT sector. 
Staying tactical within a diversified allocation. Equity 
volatility following last week’s Democratic clean sweep in the 
US elections has subsided. Questions about the pace of AI data 
centre buildout are likely to linger until Democrats take office in 
in January. Nvidia’s turmoil adds uncertainty. We remain 
broadly diversified and balanced in our core allocation across 
assets, sector and regions, while seeking tactical opportunities. 
History suggests sharp selloffs are followed by near-term 
volatility, particularly during macroeconomic stress. However, 
the balance of historical outcomes has typically shifted strongly 
to the upside over a three- to six-month horizon. Besides, 
historically, US equity markets have delivered the strongest 12-
month returns after opposition clean sweeps in the mid-terms. 
Rotation driving US IT sector recovery: As volatility eases, 
our opportunistic US IT equities idea, launched a week ago, has 
performed well, despite some significantly negative company 
specific developments. Attractive valuations support further 
upside. Investors are rotating within IT from semiconductors, 
affected by concerns over the pace of AI data centre buildout 
and Nvidia’s troubles, towards software and hardware 
providers. The latter have strong cash flows and balance sheets 
and are likely to benefit from AI monetisation. Together, they 
account for more than 60% of the IT sector (see page 4).

Remain constructive on US healthcare. US healthcare 
earnings are projected to grow almost 23% in 2027, the second-
fastest among MSCI US sectors. We see scope for further 
upside in the sector as innovation underpins long-term earnings 
growth. With the market rotating away from concentrated AI 
exposure, healthcare's stable, non-cyclical demand profile 
offers a compelling exposure as a diversifier (see page 5). 
Outside the US, Switzerland and India offer diversified 
returns. The Swiss equity market consists of 41% healthcare, 
19% financial services and 14% consumer staples, providing a 
defensive alternative to investors seeking to diversify away 
from tech-heavy exposure. The Indian equity market has been 
classified as an “anti-AI” trade. Historically narrow valuation 
premiums vs. EMs, low foreign investor positioning and c. 15% 
expected 2027 earnings growth make it an attractive diversifier. 
Strong jobs and inflation reports cement December Fed 
rate hike. The US economy added 165,000 jobs in October, 
more than twice the 70,000 consensus estimate. The 
unemployment rate fell to 4.1%. Meanwhile, headline and core 
inflation in October, at 0.4% and 0.3% m/m, respectively, 
exceeded expectations as elevated fuel prices, ‘techflation’ and 
earlier tariffs fed into directly exposed sectors, including food, 
utilities and transportation services. A healthy job market keeps 
the Fed focused on lowering inflation, cementing the prospect 
of a December rate hike, potentially followed by another in Q1 
2027. A strong economy and elevated interest rates support our 
overweight allocation to US financial sector equities. 
Locking in gains from our bullish US inflation-protected 
bonds idea. While near-term price pressures remain elevated, 
we expect inflation to ease with energy costs over the coming 
year. Republicans’ heavy losses in the US mid-term elections 
increase pressure on the Trump administration to end the Iran 
conflict. One-year US inflation expectations have slumped 
alongside oil prices. A potential slowdown in the pace of AI 
buildout is also likely to ease techflation. Meanwhile, Fed rate 
hikes should further tighten financial conditions, tempering 
price pressures. This disinflationary outlook makes the risk-
reward for US inflation-protected bonds less compelling. We 
close the idea, locking in a 4.4% gain (page 6). 
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank

WS Global CIO Office | 13 November 2026

What does history tell us about the aftermath of a single-
day sell-off of 4% or more in the S&P500 index?

Our view: The S&P500 fell 4.36% on 5 November 2026, a rare daily 
move of 3.8 standard deviations. Since 2009, there have been only

24 occasions when the index declined by 4% or more in a single

session. The immediate aftermath of such sell-offs has historically

been mixed. One-month returns ranged from around -15% to

+23%, with positive returns in 70% of cases. The weaker

outcomes were concentrated around periods of significant macro

stress, including the tail end of the 2008 Global Financial Crisis

(GFC), the 2020 COVID-19 pandemic and the 2022 monetary policy

tightening cycle. 
The historical picture becomes considerably more constructive

beyond the first month. All 24 episodes generated positive three-
month returns, ranging from roughly +0.5% to +38%, while six-
month returns were positive in 96% of cases, with gains reaching 
as much as +49%. Overall, history suggests sharp sell-offs can be 
followed by near-term volatility, particularly during periods of broader 
macro stress. However, the balance of historical outcomes has 
shifted strongly to the upside over a 3-6-month horizon. 
—  Maggie Au Yeung, Quantitative Analyst 
 o
— Francis Lim, Senior Quantitative Strategist
Where do you see the most favourable risk-reward within

the US technology sector? 
Our view: We favour high-quality Big Tech companies with 
strong balance sheets and prefer diversified exposure through 
our US Information Technology (IT) opportunistic idea. 
Rationale: The recent pullback in US semiconductor stocks has 
improved their risk-reward profile. Earnings for these companies are 
forecast to grow by 65% in 2027, the fastest rate among technology 
subsectors. Despite this outlook, recent regulatory and corporate 
governance concerns are weighing on sentiment. Potential state-
level restrictions on data centre development following the recent 
US mid-term election results could weigh on semiconductor demand 
growth. However, we believe these risks are manageable and 
continued AI adoption should support demand for AI inference. We 
prefer diversified exposure to quality semiconductor 
companies with durable demand and strong volume growth. 
To navigate volatility in the US technology sector, we favour Big Tech 
companies with strong balance sheets and resilient cash generation, 
as they are better positioned to sustain AI investments without 
relying heavily on external financing. Our US IT opportunistic idea 
combines these preferences through exposure to semiconductors, 
software and hardware, allowing investors to participate in a 
potential recovery across a broader range of earnings drivers.
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank

WS Global CIO Office | 13 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d)

Do fundamental drivers support a constructive view on the 
US healthcare sector?

Our view: We remain constructive on US healthcare, supported

by its defensive characteristics and robust earnings growth.

Rationale: US healthcare earnings are projected to grow by 22.5% 
in 2027, the second-fastest among MSCI US sectors, driven by 
improving biopharma earnings. The US healthcare sector's 
valuation gap against the broader market has been recovering from 
a steep historical discount a year ago. The sector's forward price-to-

earnings (P/E) ratio of c. 20x now exceeds the broader US market’s

c. 18.4x, but we see scope for the valuation to be supported amid

investor demand for defensive growth. The sector’s broad legislative

policy overhang has materially faded following the US election cycle,

while targeted regulatory and oversight risks remain on the watchlist.

Healthcare innovation also underpins structural growth. Wider

adoption of obesity and diabetes treatments, alongside advances in

oncology treatments, medical devices and robotic surgery, should

support long-term earnings growth. With the market rotating away

from concentrated AI exposure, healthcare's stable, non-cyclical 
demand profile presents compelling opportunities for investors 
seeking both defensiveness and growth in the near term.
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst
What is your outlook for China’s Big Tech sector? 
Our view: We remain positive on China’s Big Tech sector, 
consistent with our Overweight stance on the country’s IT and 
communication services sector. 
Rationale: Valuations across China’s technology sector remain 
inexpensive at 14.2x, with investor attention centred on whether AI-
driven revenue can offset elevated AI-related spending, which is 
currently compressing margins. Elevated AI capex and negative 
free cash flow remain key investor concerns in the Q3 earnings 
season. Top-line momentum also remains uneven: soft domestic 
consumption continues to weigh on e-commerce, advertising and 
local services, whereas gaming and cloud computing are 
delivering resilient growth. The broader narrative is steadily 
transitioning towards commercialisation, with rapid enterprise 
penetration by AI workplace agents and upcoming AI model 
launches shifting the market focus towards active deployment and 
conversion to paid usage. Evolving regulatory frameworks 
covering AI governance, agent self-regulation, tax scrutiny and 
compliance requirements remain important variables influencing 
medium-term margin trajectories. Ultimately, valuation support, 
gradual monetisation of AI capabilities and resilient core 
segments support our Overweight positioning.
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist
US healthcare sector earnings are projected to 
grow 22.5% in 2027 – the second-fastest among 
MSCI US sectors

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank

WS Global CIO Office | 13 November 2026

Why are you closing your US Treasury Inflation-Protected

Securities (TIPS) opportunistic idea? 
Our view: We are closing our opportunistic bullish US TIPS 
position, as the risk-reward profile has become less compelling.

The trade returned 4.4% since its initiation on 11 April 2025.

Rationale: We initiated an opportunistic bullish position in US TIPS

on 11 April 2025 as an oil and inflation hedge, expecting geopolitical

uncertainty to keep inflation expectations elevated. The risk-reward,

however, has since become less compelling, as hawkish Fed 
commentary, easing geopolitical risks and a potential slowdown in 
the pace of AI buildout are now likely to ease broader inflation and 
‘techflation’ pressures. We have therefore closed the opportunistic

idea, realising a gain of 4.4%.
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist
How do falling oil prices and October’s US inflation data 
affect your Fed and bond market outlook? 
Our view: Our base case envisages two further 25bps Fed hikes 
by mid-2027, with scope for cuts in H2 2027 if energy prices 
remain consistently lower and growth trajectory normalises. We 
favour income over chasing duration gains, focusing on higher-
quality corporate bonds with 3-7-year maturities. 
Rationale: Lower oil prices have a two-sided effect: they ease 
inflationary pressures and support growth by boosting purchasing 
power and lowering business costs. Cheaper energy, therefore, 
does not automatically justify Fed easing. Our base case envisages 
two further 25bps hikes by mid-2027, with scope for cuts in H2 
2027 if energy prices remain consistently lower. Sustained 
moderation in core inflation would challenge the near-term tightening 
path. However, October’s US consumer inflation print reinforced our 
assessment that each Fed meeting now remains a ‘live’ decision. 
With the US 10-year government bond yield already within our 5.0-
5.25% 3-month range, we favour income over chasing duration 
gains. Credit performance has diverged: Developed Market high-
yield bonds have outperformed both investment-grade credit and 
government bonds. Credit still offers attractive income, and we retain 
our preference for 3-7-year maturities, although heavy primary 
issuance remains a counterweight. We favour selective carry, with a 
preference for higher-quality corporate bonds. These include bonds 
issued by leading AI hyperscalers, where risk-reward has become 
more attractive. Our emphasis is on generating steady income, 
rather than relying on further declines in bond yields or spreads.
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist
While real (net-of-inflation) yields remain elevated, 
the inflation-protection function of US TIPS has 
diminished as inflation expectations ease

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank

WS Global CIO Office | 13 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d)
Does the USD’s rally have further to run?

Our view: Further upside appears limited. Stronger-than-

expected US core inflation in October and hawkish Fed

comments have pushed the US Dollar Index (DXY) above 103.

However, markets already price in more Fed tightening than we

expect. We see the DXY returning to its 101.00-102.95 range in

the near term, before gradually weakening towards our 12-

month forecast of 98.5.

Rationale: US core consumer prices rose 0.3% m/m in October,

above the 0.2% m/m expected, meeting Fed Governor Waller’s

threshold for considering a 50bps December rate hike. This pushed

US government bond yields higher, supporting the USD rally. 
However, futures now imply a Fed rate peak of 4.50-4.75% by

mid-2027, compared with our expectation of 4.25-4.50% following

two 25bps hikes, one in December and the second in H1 2027. We

expect the Fed to begin easing in H2 2027. As market expectations 
gradually converge towards our view, USD support from US bond 
yields should weaken.
Meanwhile, falling oil and petrol prices should help ease headline 
inflation in the coming months, reducing pressure for further Fed 
tightening. This reinforces our expectation of limited USD upside, 
although persistent services inflation remains a key risk.
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist
Do you expect USD/JPY to sustainably break out above 
160 in the near term?
Our view: We see USD/JPY rangebound with a downside bias, 
as growing BoJ tightening expectations and potential policy 
convergence will support gradual JPY appreciation.
Rationale: Renewed tech optimism has encouraged the re-
establishment of carry trades, providing support to the pair lately. 
Meanwhile, US core inflation beat expectations for a third 
consecutive month in October, reinforcing expectations for a 
December Fed hike. Further upside inflation surprises could widen 
the US-Japan interest rate differential and push USD/JPY higher. 
However, the risk of intervention by Japan’s Ministry of Finance 
is likely to rise materially around 160, limiting the scope for 
sustained upside. On the downside, a 25bps BoJ hike to 1.50%, 
accompanied by clear signals of consecutive hikes in March and 
June 2027, would be a significant yen-positive catalyst. A more non-
linear downside scenario would be a disorderly equity sell-off, which 
could trigger rapid JPY appreciation through carry-trade unwinding. 
We see scope for the pair to test support towards 156 in the 
coming weeks.
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist
US dollar index’s rally above 103 looks stretched

## Document 445: 「関西の景気動向」2026年11月号 人手不足とAIの急速な発展のもとで急がれる関西の人材ミスマッチの解消（PDF：1257KB）

Source PDF: all report pdf/「関西の景気動向」2026年11月号　人手不足とAIの急速な発展のもとで急がれる関西の人材ミスマッチの解消（PDF：1257KB）.pdf
Report source: JRI
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

2026 年 11 月 
株式会社 日本総合研究所 
調査部 関西経済研究センター 
http://www.jri.co.jp/report/medium/kansai/

１．景気の現状と展望 
 関西の景気は、緩やかな回復基調を維持しつつも、一部に弱い動きがみられる。企業部門では、世界的な
AI 関連需要の拡大が生産や輸出の持ち直しを支えている。設備投資は、⾧引く中東情勢の混乱の影響が懸
念される中でも、企業の収益計画が高めの水準を維持するもとで、拡大基調が続いている。一方、家計部門
では、所得環境の改善が続くものの物価高が重石となり、個人消費の回復ペースは緩慢にとどまっている。 
先行きを展望すると、米中間選挙での与党の敗北を受け、トランプ政権の通商政策における不透明感や
AI・データセンター関連投資を巡る不透明感が強まるようであれば、関西企業の設備投資意欲を減退させる恐
れがある。加えて、中東情勢の混乱を背景とした資源価格の高止まりについても、金利上昇局面における資金
調達コストの増加と相まって、企業収益の重石となる懸念がある。個人消費は、持続的な賃上げの動きが下支
えに寄与するものの、物価高による下押し圧力が根強く、回復ペースは引き続き緩慢にとどまる見通しである。

２．個別指標の動き 
（１） 輸出・生産動向 
関西の輸出額（円ベース）は増加している。国・地域別にみると、中国（含む香港）向けは足元の振れが
大きいものの、アジア（除く中国・香港）向けとともに、半導体等電子部品がけん引し、大幅に増加している。
品目別にみると、半導体等電子部品が含まれる電気機器の増勢が著しい。また、一般機械では半導体等製
造装置が押し上げに寄与している。金属及び同製品も先端材料としての需要増や、それに伴う市況の高騰を
背景に高水準で推移している。なお、WTO の定義に基づき、関西の輸出全体に占める AI 関連財の割合を
算出すると、2024～25 年の平均 23％から、足元で 30％強まで高まっており、世界的な AI 関連需要の拡
大が関西の輸出を押し上げている。

2
一方、輸出を数量面でみても、緩やかながら回復傾向にある。中国向けや米国向けは、単月の動きの振れ
が大きいが、基調としては底堅く推移している。一方、ASEAN 向けやアジア NIEs 向けは半導体等電子部品
の増加を主因に輸出金額は大きく増加しているが、輸出数量の回復は緩慢にとどまっている。

鉱工業生産は、振れを伴いながらも持ち直している。業種別にみると、世界的な AI 関連需要の拡大を受け
て半導体製造装置の増産が続く生産用機械工業が堅調に推移している。一方、電子部品・デバイス工業は
依然として低水準での推移が続いている。

（２） 企業収益・設備投資動向 
日本銀行大阪支店「短観」（９月調査、全規模・全産業）によると、関西企業全体の 2026 年度の経
常利益計画（全産業・全規模）は前年度比▲7.8％となった。資源価格の高止まりなどによる下押し圧力を
受けて減益見通しとなっているものの、コロナ禍前との比較では、引き続き高めの水準を維持している。

### Page 3

3
企業が高水準の収益を見込むもとで、関西企業は積極的な設備投資姿勢を維持している。日本銀行大
阪支店「短観」９月調査による 2026 年度の設備投資計画（全産業・全規模）は前年度比＋10.0％と、
過去（2010～2025 年度）の平均伸び率を上回る水準にある。企業規模別にみると、大企業が同＋
8.4％、中堅企業が同＋24.9％、中小企業が同＋2.4％と、いずれも増加する計画となっている。機材やソフ
トウェアの運用・調達コストの上昇などから名目の投資額は押し上げられやすい状況にあるとはいえ、中東情勢
や金利上昇といった懸念材料があるなかでも積極的な投資姿勢に変化はみられない。

（３） 雇用・所得動向 
関西の雇用環境は、人手不足を背景に良好な状態が続いている。雇用関連指標をみると、有効求人倍率
は１倍を超える水準を維持しており、完全失業率（季節調整値）は概ね 2％台後半の低位で推移している。
雇用者数は高めの水準を保っている。 
所得面をみると、府県別「毎月勤労統計調査」から推計した 7～８月の関西の一人当たり現金給与総額
は前年同期比＋4.2％と、高めの伸びを維持している。雇用者報酬を実質ベースでみると、物価高による下押
し圧力が強まるなか、7～8 月は前年同期比＋2.1％と、前期に比べ伸びが鈍化した。消費者物価は、政府

4
のエネルギ―価格の高騰対策やコメ価格の落ち
着きなどが押し下げに作用する一方で、春先以降
の原油高の影響がラグを伴って幅広い品目に波
及している。中東情勢の混乱の⾧期化により原
油価格は依然として高水準にあり、先行き、物価
の伸びは拡大傾向が続く公算が大きい。実質所
得には下押し圧力が強い状況が続く見込みであ
る。

（４） 消費動向 
関西の小売販売額をみると、百貨店、スーパー、コンビニエンスストアの販売額は緩やかに増加している。 
需要側統計（家計調査）をみると、実質ベースでみた消費支出は振れを均してみれば横ばい圏で推移し
ている。財支出は春先に生じた自動車の税制変更に伴う振れの収束後、再び伸びが抑えられているほか、サー
ビス支出は一進一退の動きとなっている。物価高が重石となるもとで、総じてみれば消費の回復の動きは緩慢に
とどまっている。

消費者マインドは、中東情勢の混乱直後の大幅
な悪化から改善傾向にある。もっとも、個別の指標を
みると、「耐久消費財の買い時判断」は他の指標に
比べ回復が遅れている。物価上昇のもとで生活必需
品以外の支出に対する慎重な姿勢が根強く、物価
高が消費者マインドの重石となっている。 
先行きの個人消費は、賃上げの持続が下支えに
寄与するものの、消費者物価の伸びが再び拡大する
もとで、回復ペースは緩慢にとどまる公算が大きい。

（５）住宅・公共投資動向 
関西の新設住宅着工戸数は、振れの大
きい分譲マンションの動きを受けて上下してい
るものの、2026 年入り後、総じてみれば横
ばい圏の推移となっている。住宅需要は、金
利の上昇や建築費の高騰による下押し圧力
を受けているものの、現在のところその影響は
限定的なものにとどまっている。

2026 年 7～９月期の公共工事請負金
額は前年同期比▲3.0％と、振れを伴いなが
らもやや減少した。もっとも、国土強靱化やイン
フラ更新需要のほか、建設コストの上昇などを
背景に、公共工事請負金額は基調としては
堅調に推移している。

（６）倒産件数 
関西の企業倒産件数は、振れを伴いながらも、均してみるとコロナ禍前を上回る水準で推移している。原材
料費の高止まりや人件費の上昇などに加え、先行き、金利上昇による資金調達コストの上昇が収益の重石に
なると予想され、倒産件数は高止まりする可能性が高い。

### Page 6

（７）観光動向 
関西の外国人延べ宿泊者数は、コロナ禍前を上回って推移しているものの、中国人旅行者の落ち込みが続
いているほか、その他の国・地域からの旅行者も伸びが鈍化しており、インバウンド需要は全体として頭打ち感が
強い。日本人の延べ宿泊者数は、台風や⾧雨などの天候不順の影響もあり、コロナ禍前の水準を割り込んで
推移している。

３．トピック:人手不足と AI の急速な発展のもとで急がれる関西の人材ミスマッチの解消 
人手不足が続くなか労働参加率は各地で上昇しており、関西でも、2020 年代入り後、人口動態による就
業者数の押し下げを上回る就業率の上昇が労働供給を下支えしている。 
もっとも、関西では女性と高齢者の就業率は他地域に比べて依然として低い水準にある。首都圏に比べ若
年層の地域定着率が低いこと、地方圏に比べ自営業や家族従業者として働き続けやすい第一次産業のウエ
ートが相対的に小さいこと、といった要因があるものの、就労環境の整備などにより就業率をさらに引き上げる余
地がある。この点、女性や高齢者の労働市場への参加を促進することが人手不足に対する有力な方策の一つ
といえる。

### Page 7

7

一方、産業構造の変化に伴い、先行き労働力の需要と供給のミスマッチが拡大すると懸念される。経済産
業省の推計によれば、AI・ロボットの活用が進む2040年には、事務職を中心に過剰感が強まるのに対し、AI・
ロボット等の利活用を担う専門職や、IT 化や AI の進化では代替しづらい現場職の人材は不足する見通しで
ある。しかも、関西は都市圏として相応の労働力人口を有するため、ミスマッチの規模は相対的に大きなものと
なる可能性がある。 
関西では、産業構造の変化に即した人材の適材適所を図ることが重要となる。そのためには、リスキリングに
関する労働者や企業の取り組みに対する公的な支援の拡充や、企業内における職種転換を含む労働市場の
流動化を進めていくことが不可欠といえる。

《AI發債引發債務可持續性擔憂》
近期公債殖利率攀高引發市場對AI融資需求以及
企業償債能力的擔憂，AI產業鏈信用利差與CDS
擴大，不過多數大型科技企業多為投資級，美國
投資級AI產業鏈信用利差水準仍未突破今年高點；
此外統計今年以來大量發債的美國投資級債發行
人(淨發行量100億美元以上)共有17家，其中17%
的發行人信評獲調升，33%被下調，50%不變，
反映債務增加尚未造成普遍的債信壓力。

基本面惡化，不過仍須留意目前總體經濟環境對於
信用評級較低的發行人而言更容易受到長期高利率
和成本壓力的影響，高低評等發行人的市場表現分
歧或將加大。

不多，2026年迄今的評級上調/下調比率為2.3倍；
雖然這一比率低於2022-2024年期間強勁的升評趨

投資級債整體信用環境維持良好。

《歐洲信用債市受法國財政疑慮波及》
歐洲信用市場雖受AI相關債券波動影響較小，但因
能源價格高企，加上近期法國主權和財政風險加劇，
歐洲信用債市十月份表現也有不小波動，尤其是非
投資級債信用利差一度擴大逾60個基點。歐洲銀行
債是各產業信用債主權風險曝險較高的類別，因歐
洲銀行多數不及美國銀行營運遍及全球且業務多元，
此外也持有不少主權債資產，十月份市場表現也反
映此一狀況，主權債的波動更加重了歐洲銀行債的
跌幅，法國銀行債利差擴大幅度最為明顯，所幸法
國主要銀行包括法國巴黎銀行以及興業銀行3Q26獲
利狀況良好，即使經營環境惡化，從不良貸款率與

信用風險成本等資產品質指標來看法國巴黎銀行持
平前季、法國興業銀行僅微幅上升。
目前市場關注的焦點仍是法國財政整頓的路徑，法
國目前的債務負擔約佔GDP的118%至119%，高盛
預計，如果沒有更有效的財政調整，到2030年初負
債比率將進一步上升至接近125%，如要穩定債務
佔GDP的比重，法國需要實現約佔GDP 1%的財政
盈餘，但目前的財政赤字約佔2.5% GDP。法國政
府當局可能在年底前通過一項撙節的預算案，若沒
通過法國主權債與信用債可能進一步惡化，但即使
通過法國主權債利率要大幅回落的機會仍不大，可
能要到2027年法國大選後主權債才有機會趨於穩定，
屆時法國銀行債或有布局機會。
歐洲可供吸收損失銀行債信用利差(基點)
資料來源：高盛，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10

重點摘要
• 高利率持續下信用基本面都
已 出 現分 化，如CCC等級的
償債能力指標顯著疲弱
• 衡量整體債信風險仍是歐洲
較美國為高
《非投資級債美國仍優於歐洲》
儘管目前不論是美國還是歐洲的非投資級債在高利
率持續下信用基本面都已出現分化，如CCC等級的
償債能力指標顯著疲弱，但衡量整體債信風險仍是
歐洲較美國為高；原因包括歐洲的債務到期牆較美
國明顯，且美國非投資級債2028年前到期的債券分
佈在相對廣泛的行業，而當前的賤售債券主要集中
在媒體、原物料與電信、非必需消費等特定領域，
上述較少重疊降低落入賤售債券利差區間的發行人
同時面臨債務到期後償債或再融資令違約風險增加
的可能。另一方面，歐洲非投資級債的到期牆更為
陡峭，且市場利率與2028年前到期債券的票面利率

差距大於美非投資級債，反映再融資壓力更鉅，尤
其是歐洲CCC等級。
分析歐洲非投資級債各產業債務到期狀況可發現
2028年前到期債務產業相對集中，且易受景氣影響
的非必需消費在到期債務與賤售債券都佔有一定比
例，另外槓桿率本就較高的電信業佔2028年前到期
債務9%，融資成本大幅上漲恐令這些發行人債信體
質進一步惡化。
資料來源：高盛，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10
資料來源：高盛，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10

投資風格轉換，資金由成長型轉向防禦或分紅型產業
《投資關鍵-10月中國股市黯淡，防禦型產業當道》
10月中國股市疲軟，日均成交量能萎縮，投資情緒
保守。產業表現顯著風格轉換，資金由成長型轉向
防禦或分紅型產業，能源、必需消費和金融產業表
現最佳。儘管市場上修中國股市科技股獲利預期，
然信息技術指數跟隨全球科技重挫而同步回調。

重點摘要
3
• 五 中 全 會 公 報 隱 含 官 方
對 完 成 年 度 經 濟 目 標 具
相 當 信 心 ， 短 期 推 出 大
規 模 景 氣 刺 激 政 策 可 能
性降低。
• 五 中 全 會 前 後 顯 示 本 輪
政 治 整 頓 力 度 短 期 有 所
降溫， 但 整 體 水 準 仍 明
顯高於歷史均值。
《投資關鍵-反腐短暫趨緩，關注4Q景氣能否達標》
中國第三季實質GDP年增4.5%，前三季累計年增
4.6%，位居官方全年4.5%至5.0%目標區間下緣，
整體延續外強內弱格局。出口強勁，加上政策刺激
提供經濟底部支撐，然投資與消費兩大內需引擎同
步降溫，內生需求不足的結構性問題未有效改善。
展望後市，官方將擴大超長期特別國債與地方政府

專項債發行規模，加快重大基建項目落地，並持續
透過房地產去庫存、城中村改造等定向措施穩定內
需，搭配結構性寬鬆政策引導融資成本下行。惟五
中全會公報強調「確保完成全年經濟社會發展主要
目標任務」來看，官方對完成年度成長目標具相當
信心，因此第四季GDP年增率僅需維持約4.5%至
4.6%，短期推出大規模景氣刺激政策可能性降低。
本次五中全會主要聚焦黨的治理體系、監督機制與
自我革命等議題，並未釋出新一輪大規模財政或貨
幣刺激訊號。此外，觀察高盛編製的反腐敗代理指
標，五中全會前後雖略有回落，顯示本輪政治整頓
力度短期有所降溫，但整體水準仍明顯高於歷史均
值。隨著2027年新一屆黨領導層改組逐步接近，反
腐與監管力道仍有再度升溫的可能。在此背景下，
地方官員風險偏好與投資意願恐持續受到抑制，進
而削弱政策寬鬆措施向實體經濟的傳導效果，使中
國經濟仍面臨政策支持與內生需求不足並存的挑戰。
留意反貪腐力道若再度上升將壓抑政策傳導效果
資料來源：高盛，2026/10/30。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11/2。

《投資關鍵-回測顯示美期中選後短線有反彈契機》
美國11/3將舉行參眾兩院的國會選舉，除視為對
當任總統的支持度檢驗之一，參眾兩院的黨派席次
也可能影響接下來政策推行速度。
回顧過去美股期中選舉期間，新興市場選前大多表
現壓抑，惟選後大多繳出正報酬佳績，上漲比率超
越五成。若以參眾兩院控制權皆翻轉的兩個年度
1994年、2006年來看，1944年因美聯準會啟動快
速升息循環，加上墨西哥金融危機爆發，導致選後
新興市場進入近一年的空頭走勢。然2006年即便
也是分裂政府，全球宏觀環境正受惠全球貿易繁榮、
商品超級周期推動經濟成長，新興市場選後12個

月累積報酬相當亮眼。

重點摘要
5
• 同樣面臨美國政治版圖翻
轉，新興市場指數表現卻
顯著差異，意味選後更取
決於景氣與獲利週期，而
非國會席次變化本身。
• 過去美股期中選舉前。台
股的平均表現最為壓抑，
但選後反彈幅度亦最為顯
著，且正報酬機率維持在
較高水準。
比較1994年與2006年同樣面臨美國政治版圖翻轉，
指數表現卻顯著差異，意味選後更取決於景氣與獲
利週期，而非國會席次變化本身。
進一步檢視1990年以來美國期中選舉的各市場表
現，中國上證指數受自身政策與景氣循環影響較深，
與美國期中選舉關聯性相對有限外，多數市場皆呈
現選前承壓、選後回升的特徵。其中，台股於期中
選舉前的平均表現最為壓抑，但選後反彈幅度亦最
為顯著，且正報酬機率維持在較高水準。或反應美

國政治不確定性於選後下降，市場焦點重新回歸景
氣與企業獲利基本面，帶動資金自美國逐步向Beta
值較高的市場擴散。進一步而言，若相關國家具備
較穩健的經濟基本面、企業獲利動能或產業成長題
材支撐，則更有機會在風險偏好回升與資金重新配
置的過程中，成為資金優先流入的受惠對象
資料來源：Bloomberg，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/28。

資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
重點摘要
6
• 美期中選對新興市場影響
較大的政策面向主要包括
關稅與貿易政策、債務上
限議題，以及政府對資料
中心建設與AI相關投資的
態度。
• 即使選後對資料中心的監
管聲浪升高，政策方向更
可能聚焦於強化治理框架
與技術管制，而非限制境
內AI投資或資料中心建設。
《投資關鍵-期中選舉後關注政策推動能力的變遷》
另一個觀察美國期中選舉後政黨版圖變化對新興市
場影響的角度，在於其對政策推動能力的改變。美
期中選對新興市場影響較大的政策面向主要包括關
稅與貿易政策、債務上限議題，以及政府對資料中
心建設與AI相關投資的態度。
首先，在關稅與貿易政策方面，由於關稅、地緣政
治及貿易談判等政策多屬行政權限範疇，政策方向
仍由白宮主導，且部分政策工具並不高度依賴國會

授權，因此政策波動性仍需關注。
其次，債務上限與財政融資需求則是影響資金流向
的重要觀察指標。國會僵局或限制積極的財政政策，
但也可能讓債務上限僵局上演，擾動市場風險偏好。
最後，近期美國各地對資料中心的反對聲浪升溫，
主要集中耗電、用水及對當地生活環境的影響。然
而，資料中心能否順利興建，關鍵仍掌握在州政府、
公共事業委員會及地方政府手中，因其負責電力、
水資源、土地分區與稅務優惠等審批事項。因此，
影響AI基建進度的主要變數未必是聯邦層級的期中
選舉，而更可能來自地方政府的政策取向。另一方
面，美國已將AI視為攸關國家競爭力、經濟成長與
國家安全的關鍵基礎設施，因此即使選後監管聲浪
升高，政策方向更可能聚焦於強化治理框架與技術
管制，而非限制境內AI投資或資料中心建設。換言
之，選後最大的差異較可能體現在建設進度與行政
程序，而非AI投資趨勢本身出現明顯逆轉。對以科
技硬體與AI供應鏈為主的新興亞洲市場而言，企業
獲利與資本支出動能仍將是比國會版圖變化更重要
的驅動因素。

6月底啟動三大超級計畫後，韓國政府
加快推出相關配套措施，宣佈設立5兆
韓元半導體專項基金，重點投資芯片材
料、零組件企業以及無晶圓廠半導體企
業，並提供相關供應商總額5兆韓元貿
易融資。此外啟動規模1兆韓元為期10
年計畫，促進大企業和較小供應商合作
如半導體研發、測試及生產等領域。再
者費外成為對經濟貢獻的推手之一。
重點摘要
7
• 期中選舉落幕後，焦點重
回美伊戰事若開打，油價
回升恐再度引發高利環境
的擔憂。
《投資關鍵-以長線投資視野聚焦穩健獲利標的》
展望後市，新興市場第四季仍將面臨波動升溫的環
境。 從歷史經驗與政策面觀察，美國期中選舉後
政治不確定性雖有望下降，但市場焦點最終仍將回
歸總體經濟、企業獲利與流動性環境。據傳美以軍

方擬定在美國期中選舉後再攻擊伊朗，而這可能再
度推升油價，進而引發市場對高利環境的擔憂。
基本面方面，市場預估新興市場經濟成長率將由
2026年第三季的3.4%升至第四季的4.4%，顯示整
體景氣仍具韌性。然而，高利率環境維持時間長於
預期，正考驗各國內需與投資動能；同時，已開發
國家央行偏鷹立場未見明顯鬆動，也使新興市場進
一步降息與推動寬鬆政策的空間受到壓縮。市場對
2027年新興市場景氣與企業獲利的預期差異恐將
擴大，資產表現也將更加分化。
台灣涵蓋晶圓代工、IC設計、先進封裝、AI伺服器、
PCB及網通設備等完整AI供應鏈。多層次且廣泛的
產業布局，不僅有助於分散單一產業景氣波動風險，
也使AI投資動能更全面傳導至企業獲利與出口表現，
資料來源：Bloomberg，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/28。

在「實體人工智慧」的第二支柱下，韓
國政府的目標是將韓國打造為全球人工
智慧機器人領域的前三名。重點領域包
括：1）製造業轉型為人工智慧；2）保
障核心人工智慧技術與資料安全；3）
建立區域性大規模生產中心。在另一份
新聞稿中，韓國政府指出，將投資 20
兆韓元用於人工智慧製造業，目標是創
造 100 兆韓元的附加價值。
重點摘要
8
• 台股或因美期中選舉落幕
後重啟反彈，但美伊戰事
的開打可能再度壓抑台股
表現，預計最壞情境可留
意年線支撐，但來年第一
季有望迎更穩健表現。
進而帶動台灣在主要新興市場中持續名列獲利預期
上修幅度領先族群。
隨著市場對AI投資效益與變現能力的檢驗日趨嚴格，
具備實質獲利支撐與產業擴散效益的市場，可望更

受資金青睞，而台股憑藉完整供應鏈優勢與穩健的
獲利上修趨勢，仍具相對吸引力。本行認為台股或
因美期中選舉落幕後重啟反彈，但美伊戰事的開打
可能再度壓抑台股表現，預計最壞情境可留意年線
支撐，但來年第一季有望迎更穩健表現。
韓國股市方面， 10 月 中 旬 受 長 鑫 存儲 宣 布 將 於
2026年底推出新一代DDR5 RDIMM伺服器記憶體
影響，拖累記憶體族群股價表現。本行認為，新產
品能否形成實質供給仍有賴良率提升及客戶採用進
度驗證。然而，此事件也凸顯韓股產業結構相對集
中於記憶體與半導體領域，使指數表現較容易受到
單一產業或個別企業消息影響。疊加市場期待韓國
政府推動的資本市場改革尚未取得實質進展，也導
致市場對過去低估的韓股評價重估行情有所失望，
除外資賣壓未歇外，韓股各投資類別資金流向，9
月較大股市資金流入來自三星與海力士的企業回購，
然企業回購已告一段落。預計韓股行情從單邊上漲
轉為區間震盪，建議投資建議暫為觀望。
資料來源：MS，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10。

9
• 中國10月景氣數據轉弱，
短期政策重心可望回歸穩
增長，但全年目標達成有

中國股市方面，儘管過去五中全會通常以審議下一
個五年規劃建議為主要任務，然由於四中全會已通
過十五五規劃，因此今年的五中全會聚焦國家安全
主軸並不意外。隨五中全會已遞補10位政治局委
員，高盛的反貪腐指數略下滑，短線政治整頓力度
降溫。中國10月官方製造業與服務業景氣領指標
再度低預期，同步跌破榮枯線，短線官方政策焦點
可能會重回穩增長，但由於達全年官方目標並不困
難，研判穩增長偏向支撐而非大力度刺激，政策傾
斜高端製造看來仍是主軸。
在整個中國經濟結構持續處外需韌性與內需疲弱的
拉鋸下，長線中國股市投資邏輯維持選股不選市，
聚焦受惠AI相關供應鏈兼國產替代政策扶植的科技
族群、稀缺原物料及高附加出口價值等產業。但由
於短期中國科技股同步受海外科技股回調而整理，
建議嚴控持股比重，並可適時增持有高股息分紅防
禦的金融產業來平衡波動風險。

資產 4Q26 看法 投資展望
核心消費 中立 本行維持產業上下半年營收成長48：52的溫和成長預期，消費產業在旺
季前呈現「量能可望持平、獲利品質分化」，同時零售商備貨行為與終
端消費需求仍支持到貨量的基本盤，但消費者實際支出意願可能受到油
價／利率上升抑制，戰術性減碼受油價與信心承壓較重的旅遊休閒類股
4Q26預計呈「量能可望維持、獲利偏弱」格局
資料來源：美國銀行2026/10/15，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/28。

消費產業投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/10/29
BofA信用卡數據9月卡片消費YoY回升、零售銷售續增(惟
剔除汽油站僅+0.2%),延續8月自7月疲弱中的反彈,為本行
對量販/民生通路保持加碼提供量能基準。
總卡片消費YoY成長率於6-8月連續降溫(6月6.3%→7月
5.0%→8月4.5%),剔除油品後同步走緩
(5.6%→4.3%→3.7%)，主因6月世界盃與線上促銷時序錯
位帶來的高基期效應消退。9月YoY回升至6.2%、剔除油品
為5.1%(BofA指出皆為近四年第三強),惟季調後MoM僅
+0.2%(7月-0.2%、8月+0.9%),剔除油品後幾乎持平,顯示
YoY反彈部分來自油價推升,月度動能趨於溫和。投資意涵：
量能未見明顯失速，不支持對消費產業全面削減曝險,宜改
採結構性次產業輪動

《「K型」分化持續收斂，中低所得消費有收入基礎》
低所得家戶的消費與稅後薪資成長自7月起皆居三分位
（Tercile）組之首，高所得（不含前5%）已被中、低所
得超越。三分位組中，低所得家戶的消費與薪資成長皆最
強；前5%仍是消費成長最快的族群，但已較高點放緩。
各組消費成長均高於稅後薪資成長約1個百分點（pp）。

9月各所得階層信用消費成長率 vs. 稅後薪資成長率

零售客流量表
5
民生必需品：P&G有機成長 +4.0%中，銷量貢獻 +2.4%，
價格貢獻有限；引用的NielsenIQ資料也顯示家庭與個人

餐飲：2Q26同店銷售呈現明顯分化，本行預估客流領先
的連鎖與咖啡系統維持正成長，倚賴折價促銷的速食與披
薩鏈許多為負成長。在外食價格比居家食品高出約1.7個
百分點的環境下，客流與價格的取捨將是4Q26餐飲評等
的主要變數。
《成本與定價動能》
9月CPI顯示，整體CPI月增 +0.6%、年增 +3.7%，核心
CPI年增 +2.6%；能源年增 +20.4%、汽油年增 +34.2%，

是整體通膨上行的主因。對核心消費而言，關鍵在於食品
與能源的分離：居家食品月增 −0.1%、年增 +1.8%，外
食年增 +3.5%。
對毛利的含意：能源成本上升而零售端食品價格溫和，代
表核心消費業者較難以轉嫁成本，壓力更可能先反映在毛
利率而非銷售額。P&G在指引中揭露本財年約14億美元
稅後成本逆風（含其他逆風合計約每股0.79美元，約拖累
核心EPS成長11%），為公司自行揭露數字，本行未重新
計算。Kimberly-Clark則預期2026年有機銷售成長約落
後 所 屬 品 類 加 權 成 長 100bp ， 顯示在品類成長僅約
+1.7% 的環境下價格與組合的操作空間有限。成長動能
偏向「量」而非「價」；此結構有利於營收穩定，但對毛
利率的支撐較弱。

## Document 522: 中国･５中全会で党内統治の引き締め強化も、景気には不透明感 ～習近平指導部の４期目入りを見据える一方、景気低迷と株安への対応が課題に～

Source PDF: all report pdf/中国･５中全会で党内統治の引き締め強化も、景気には不透明感 ～習近平指導部の４期目入りを見据える一方、景気低迷と株安への対応が課題に～.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年 11 月 2 日

1/4 
中国･５中全会で党内統治の引き締め強化も、景気には不透明感 
～習近平指導部の４期目入りを見据える一方、景気低迷と株安への対応が課題に～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ 中国では、10 月 26～29 日に開催された中国共産党の５中全会において、党内統治の引き締
めや反腐敗の強化を柱とする決定が採択された。11 月１日に公表された決定の全文では、党
への忠誠や幹部に対する監督の徹底が強調されるなど、2027 年の党大会での習近平指導部の
４期目入りを見据え、党組織に対する統制を一段と強化する狙いがうかがえる。 
○ 一方、中国経済を巡っては、当局が内需喚起に向けた財政・金融支援策を打ち出しているも
のの、10 月の製造業ＰＭＩは政府統計（49.3）と民間統計（52.0）で明暗が分かれた。非製
造業ＰＭＩ（49.7）も50 を下回るなど、企業マインドは依然として力強さを欠いている。 
○ さらに、原油高によるコスト上昇や不動産開発企業の経営環境悪化などが株式相場の重しと
なっている。こうしたなか、当局による市場支援への期待が高まっているものの、政策対応
が景気や金融市場の本格的な改善につながるかは不透明であり、先行きの動向には引き続き
注意が必要である。

【５中全会では、習近平指導部の４期目入りを見据え、党内統治の引き締め強化を図った】 
中国では、10 月 26 日から 29 日までの日程で中国共産党の第 20 期中央委員会第５回全体会議
（５中全会）が開催された（注１）。主要議題は「全面的かつ厳格な党内統治（全面従厳治党）」と
され、2027 年に開催予定の中国共産党第21 回全国代表大会（第 21 回党大会）で習近平指導部の４
期目入りが視野に入るなか、その『前哨戦』として注目された。会議では、習氏が中央政治局を代
表して行った活動報告を聴取・討議し、「全面的かつ厳格な党内統治を持続的に推進するうえでの
若干の重大問題に関する決定」を採択した。同決定には、党の規律、幹部の選抜・監督、組織運営、
反腐敗の強化といった内容が盛り込まれた。中央の指示を地方や各部門まで徹底し、政治的な忠誠
と実務上の遂行能力を幹部管理に反映させるとともに、組織的な監督と責任追及の仕組みを整える
方針が示された。こうした決定の背景には、「一時みられた党の指導力の弱体化・形骸化・希薄化と

注１ 10 月30 日付レポート「中共５中全会閉幕、次期党大会を見据えて党・軍への統制強化を加速」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/672289.html）

### Page 2

2/4 
いう厳しい現実を直視した」とする危機感があったとみられる。 
その後、11 月１日に公表された内決定の全文では、党への忠誠を重視する姿勢があらためて示さ
れるとともに、党内の『不純物やウイルス』を一掃すべく、反腐敗運動を引き続き強力に展開する
方針が示された。そのうえで、党員全体としては健全であると評価しつつも、思想面や組織面、仕
事ぶりにおける不純さの問題が根本的に解決されていないと指摘した。そして、党への忠誠心を浸
透させる取り組みの強化、日常業務や個人の言動、交友関係、家族の価値観を通じて評価される『党
性』に基づく幹部登用、『自己革命』を推進する必要性を強調した。具体的には、幹部の個人資産な
どに関する申告内容の審査を厳格化するとともに、家族の資産や支出が正当な所得に見合わない事
例の調査を含め、綿密かつ継続的な監督を行うとした。幹部の家族による事業活動についても、定
期的な監督の対象とすることを求めている。さらに、名義貸しによる株式保有や、暗号資産、ブロ
ックチェーン技術、国境を越えた資金移動などを利用した汚職の取り締まりを強化する方針も示さ
れた。 
そのうえで、習氏を党の『核心』とする地位を断固として守るとともに、思想、政治、行動の各
方面で最高指導部と完全に足並みを揃えることを要求した。習氏は、決議について説明した演説で
も、「全面的かつ厳格な党内統治を粘り強く推進し、党の健全性をむしばむ『ウイルス』を絶えず排
除することで、党の本質や理念を維持し、『紅色の国（中国）』を次世代に引き継ぐことができる」
との考えを示した。背景には、改革・解放後に生まれた若い世代の党幹部が増えるなか、思想面な
どで「新たな問題が生じている」との認識が広がっていることが影響している模様である。こうし
たことから、今回の５中全会には、習近平指導部が４期目入りを前に、党組織に対する統制を一段
と強化する狙いがあったと考えられる。

【当局による内需喚起の取り組みにもかかわらず、足元の企業マインドは依然として厳しい】 
一方で、足元の中国経済を巡っては、外需やハイテク関連を中心とする製造業をけん引役に景気
に持ち直しの動きがみられる一方、内需は力強さを欠く推移をみせている。こうしたなか、５中全
会では、マクロ政策の活用と内需拡大を求めるとともに、2026 年の経済・社会発展目標を達成する
方針が再確認された。中国ではここ数ヵ月、内需喚起に向けた取り組みを進める動きがみられる。
国務院は７月、同国で初めて個人消費に焦点を当てた「消費拡大の５ヵ年計画」を公表した。同計
画では、2030 年の小売売上高（社会消費品小売総額）の規模を 2025 年（50.1 兆元）に比べて約２
割（60 兆元前後）引き上げるべく、消費構造の高度化や家計所得の引き上げを目指すとした。加え
て、共産党は７月末に開催した中央政治局会議において、インフラ関連など財政支出の進捗加速に
よる景気下支えを図る方針を示した。これを受けて、政府・金融当局は８月以降、内需促進を目的
とする新たな財政・金融支援策を発表しており、今後はその効果の行方が注目される。

### Page 3

3/4 
ただし、足元においては、依然として企業を取り巻く環境は厳しい状況が続いている。国家統計
局が公表した 10 月の製造業ＰＭＩ（購買
担当者景況感）は 49.3 となり、前月
（50.1）から▲0.8pt 低下して２ヵ月ぶ
りに好不況の分かれ目となる 50 を下回
る水準となった（図１）。足元の生産動向
